Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦电信领域的图推荐系统（graph-based recommendations），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及在轨部署或星上推理。虽然文中提到了"轻量GNN"（lightweight GNN）和计算效率优化，但这是针对图推荐系统的通用效率问题，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图推荐系统（graph-based recommendations）；应用场景：电信领域（telecommunications domain）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 两阶段扩散框架：第一阶段通过图粗化（graph coarsening）将原始大图聚合为社区（communities），然后使用标签传播算法（LPA）或图神经网络（GNN）在粗化图上传播标签得到粗粒度预测；第二阶段在子图内再次使用LPA生成最终推荐。

Lightweight method: 图粗化（graph coarsening）作为一种自适应图缩减技术，通过减少图规模来降低计算成本。文中提到在第一阶段使用轻量GNN（lightweight GNN），但摘要未说明具体轻量化手段（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到该方法在"scalability, latency, and recommendation quality"之间取得了平衡，但未给出具体延迟或实时性数据。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实世界电信数据集（real-world telecommunications dataset）；评价指标：NDCG@5（Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain@5）。

Experiments: 两阶段均使用LPA时，相比全图LPA基线NDCG@5提升+24%；在第一阶段引入轻量GNN后NDCG@5进一步提升超过+50%，但训练和推理时间显著增加。通过消融实验量化了质量与效率之间的权衡。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 轻量GNN的具体架构和参数量未说明；3. 未提供推理延迟、FLOPs等效率指标的量化数据；4. 仅在电信推荐场景验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：1. "图粗化"（graph coarsening）思想——在遥感场景中，如果涉及图结构数据（如场景图理解或多目标关系建模），可考虑类似的自适应图缩减策略；2. 两阶段"粗粒度预测+细粒度精调"的级联思路，可类比遥感目标检测中的"粗检测+细分类"或"候选区域生成+精定位"范式；3. 文中对"质量-效率权衡"的量化分析方法值得借鉴，但需迁移到遥感目标检测的精度-速度-参数量权衡分析中。

Abstract: Graph-based recommendations are widely adopted in real-world industrial applications. However, graphs in these systems often reach a massive scale, posing notable scalability and efficiency challenges. This requires techniques that can effectively balance predictive quality with computational cost. One promising approach is graph coarsening, an adaptive graph reduction technique that offers a way to systematically construct smaller, yet structurally representative, versions of the original large-scale graphs.
  In this work, we propose a flexible two-stage diffusion framework that combines graph coarsening with multi-step label propagation in the telecommunications domain. Domain-specific heuristics are applied to first aggregate nodes into meaningful communities, reducing graph size while preserving essential business-relevant relationships. An initial diffusion process done by a Label Propagation Algorithm (LPA) or a Graph Neural Network (GNN) propagates labels across the coarsened graph to produce coarse-grained predictions. Finally, a second LPA within subgraphs generates the final recommendations for individual users.
  On a real-world telecommunications dataset, when using LPA in both stages, our method achieves up to +24% NDCG@5 over the full-graph LPA baseline. Incorporating a lightweight GNN in the first stage further boosts NDCG@5 by more than 50%, but requires substantial training and inference time. Through extensive experiments and a detailed ablation, we quantify these trade-offs and demonstrate that our coarsening-driven approach delivers an optimal balance between scalability, latency, and recommendation quality.

</details>


### [165] [Synthetic Speech, Real Signal: Paralinguistic Preservation and Cross-Lingual Augmentation via Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22304)
*Roseline Polle,Owen Parsons,George Fairs,Luis Miguel San Martin Fernandez,Cole Looney,Xiaoliang Wu,Alexandra Livia Georgescu,Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语音克隆（voice cloning）在副语言任务（paralinguistic tasks）中的数据增强效果，重点评估其对临床语音信号（如抑郁、焦虑检测）的保持能力，并探索跨语言迁移场景下的应用。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦语音领域的副语言任务数据增强（voice cloning for paralinguistic tasks），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。属于完全不同的模态（语音 vs. 视觉/遥感图像）和任务领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务类型：语音副语言任务（paralinguistic tasks），包括抑郁检测、焦虑检测等临床任务。传感器/数据模态：语音信号（speech）。目标类别：临床语音中的副语言信号。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。涉及8种语音克隆模型（voice cloning models），但未列出具体名称。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：公开数据集和临床数据集（public and clinical datasets），以及英语临床语音和日语真实语音。评价指标：WER（词错误率）、SS（说话人相似度）用于评估语音克隆质量；下游任务性能用于评估副语言信号保持能力。

Experiments: 在5个副语言任务上对8种语音克隆模型进行基准测试，发现大多数模型能保持信号但有一定退化。跨语言实验显示，在英语临床语音上克隆后用于日语训练，效果优于原始跨语言迁移方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未讨论语音克隆对副语言信号的保持机制，也未涉及模型效率问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于语音领域，与遥感图像目标检测和模型轻量化在模态、任务、方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation in speech is common practice for linguistic tasks like ASR, but has seen far less work for paralinguistic ones, especially clinical tasks where labelled data is expensive and some patient groups are underrepresented. Voice cloning is one such augmentation approach, but is typically evaluated on speech intelligibility (WER) or speaker similarity (SS) rather than on downstream performance, and it remains unclear whether these preserve the paralinguistic signal such tasks depend on. We benchmark eight voice cloning models on five paralinguistic tasks across public and clinical datasets, showing most preserve signal with modest degradation. We then clone English clinical speech into Japanese and find that training on cloned data outperforms raw cross-lingual transfer for depression and anxiety detection on real Japanese speech, suggesting voice cloning is a promising direction for augmenting clinical speech data in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [166] [Evolution-Aware MSA Reasoning for Subsampling via Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22314)
*Zhangzhi Xiong,Minzhang Li,Haotian Yu,Sixian Shen,Kexin Zhang,Mingrui Li,Jie Zheng,Kewei Tu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于亲和传播因子图的MSA子采样方法AP-REASONER，用于蛋白质结构预测中的多序列比对子集优化选择。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是蛋白质语言模型中的多序列比对(MSA)子采样问题，属于计算生物学/蛋白质结构预测领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：MSA子采样优化；应用场景：蛋白质结构预测（长程接触预测、构象集成预测）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: AP-REASONER：基于亲和传播(Affinity Propagation)的因子图方法，包含进化感知一元因子(evolution-aware unary factors)、示例一致性因子(exemplar-consistency factors)和两个控制旋钮(control knobs)，通过消息传递(message passing)进行因子图推理。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称；下游任务：长程接触预测(long-range contact prediction)、构象集成预测(conformational ensemble prediction)。

Experiments: 在长程接触预测和构象集成预测任务上，AP-REASONER优于基线子采样方法(baseline subsamplers)，在结构敏感下游任务上表现更好，并能够可控地恢复替代蛋白质构象。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与本研究方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。因子图推理和消息传递的优化框架在理论上可类比为一种"选择/采样"策略，但应用场景（蛋白质序列 vs. 遥感图像）和方法目标（保留进化信息 vs. 轻量化目标检测）差异巨大，不建议直接借鉴。

Abstract: Multiple Sequence Alignments (MSAs) provide protein language models with explicit evolutionary context, but their large depth makes subsampling unavoidable under limited token budgets. Existing strategies, including random selection, identity-based filtering, and diversity-driven sampling, are effective heuristics, yet provide limited control over the evolutionary signals retained in the subset. In this work, we recast MSA subsampling as an explicit optimization problem, where key evolutionary measures, including query identity and diversity, are treated as controllable objectives. Building on this view, we introduce AP-REASONER, an Affinity-Propagation-based factor-graph approach. With evolution-aware unary factors, exemplar-consistency factors, and two control knobs, AP-REASONER performs factor-graph reasoning through message passing to infer a fixed-budget MSA subset. Experiments on long-range contact prediction and conformational ensemble prediction show that AP-REASONER outperforms baseline subsamplers on structure-sensitive downstream tasks and enables controllable recovery of alternative protein conformations. These results highlight the value of modeling MSA subsampling as a controllable optimization problem, where factor-graph reasoning offers an effective alternative to heuristic selection.

</details>


### [167] [Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation via Byte-Prefix Marginalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22334)
*Hao Wang,Kun Yuan,Wenlin Zhong,Minglei Zhang,Han Xiao,Ming Sun,Honggang Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Byte-Prefix Marginalization (BPM)方法，解决跨tokenizer的on-policy蒸馏中教师模型概率分布向学生词汇表对齐的问题，在数学和编程benchmark上提升性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的跨tokenizer知识蒸馏方法，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型的知识蒸馏（on-policy distillation）；应用场景：数学推理、编程；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 教师模型：Qwen3-32B, GLM-Z1-9B-0414, MiniMax-M2.7；学生模型：摘要未明确说明具体架构，但为compact student；核心方法：Byte-Prefix Marginalization (BPM)用于跨tokenizer概率对齐。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation），但这是LLM领域的蒸馏方法，与遥感模型轻量化无关。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：六个数学和编程benchmark（具体名称摘要未列出）；评价指标：avg@8。

Experiments: 在Qwen3-32B、GLM-Z1-9B-0414、MiniMax-M2.7作为教师模型时，BPM在六个benchmark上avg@8比最强baseline提升3.7-6.6个点。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 方法针对LLM文本tokenizer设计，无法直接迁移到视觉或遥感图像任务；3. 未讨论计算效率或部署开销。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发是：如果未来在遥感领域做跨模态/跨架构的蒸馏（例如从大视觉模型蒸馏到轻量检测模型），BPM的"字节前缀对齐"思想在理论上可能有启发，但遥感图像是连续像素空间而非离散token空间，直接迁移困难。

Abstract: Open-weight language models from different families exhibit complementary capabilities, motivating their consolidation into a compact student through on-policy distillation (OPD). However, full-vocabulary OPD typically assumes a shared tokenizer, while existing cross-tokenizer methods may discard teacher probability mass or assign it to student tokens with unrelated content. We introduce Byte-Prefix Marginalization (BPM), which re-expresses the teacher's next-token distribution over the student vocabulary in a shared byte space. Specifically, BPM assigns each teacher token's probability to the longest student token whose byte representation is a prefix of the teacher token's bytes, aggregates mass mapped to the same student token, and places otherwise unmatched mass in an explicit residual category. This produces a vocabulary-complete, byte-aligned, and mass-preserving target for dense OPD. The target exactly recovers the teacher-induced byte-prefix marginal when the relevant prefix does not span multiple teacher tokens (a condition satisfied at more than 99% of training positions) and uses a mass-preserving, chain-factorized lower bound otherwise. Across Qwen3-32B, GLM-Z1-9B-0414, and MiniMax-M2.7 as teachers, BPM consistently outperforms current cross-tokenizer methods on six mathematics and programming benchmarks, improving six-benchmark avg@8 by 3.7-6.6 points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Binary Rooftop Mapping: A Four-Class Deep Learning Framework for Green Roof Potential Assessment from Open Swiss Geospatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22342)
*Htet Yamin Ko Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于改进深度卷积神经网络的屋顶分类框架，利用高分辨率航拍影像和屋顶坡度信息，将屋顶分为四类（现有绿屋顶、适合安装绿屋顶的屋顶、太阳能板屋顶、不适合绿化的平屋顶），服务于城市热岛缓解和绿色基础设施规划。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。虽然使用了航拍影像（aerial imagery）和数字表面模型，但任务为屋顶分类（rooftop classification），而非目标检测（object detection）；不涉及遥感卫星在轨处理；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、边缘部署等任何压缩或加速技术；不涉及舰船/飞机/车辆等典型遥感目标检测对象。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：屋顶语义分类（四分类）；应用场景：城市气候适应、绿色基础设施规划、城市热岛缓解；传感器类型：高分辨率航拍影像（orthophotos）+ 数字表面模型（DSM）；目标类别：现有绿屋顶、适合绿化的屋顶、太阳能板屋顶、不适合绿化的平屋顶。不涉及目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 基于Roofpedia改进的深度卷积神经网络（modified deep CNN），具体架构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。该框架为离线城市尺度分析工具，不面向星上或边缘实时推理场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Swisstopo公开数据（SWISSIMAGE正射影像、swissSURFACE3D高程数据、swissTLM3D建筑足迹）；应用城市：瑞士伯尔尼；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标（如准确率、IoU等）。

Experiments: 在伯尔尼市应用，将屋顶分为四类，识别了绿屋顶扩展的现实机会。摘要未提供与其他方法的定量对比或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 1. 任务为分类而非检测，与目标检测方向无关；2. 不涉及任何轻量化或模型压缩技术；3. 不面向在轨/边缘部署场景；4. 仅在一个城市验证，泛化性未充分证明；5. 无定量性能指标报告。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的点是：该工作融合了航拍影像与DSM坡度信息作为多模态输入，这种多源数据融合思路在遥感目标检测中也可借鉴（如光学+SAR融合），但与本课题的轻量化在轨检测核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: The development of effective urban climate adaptation strategies requires comprehensive spatial information on rooftops and buildings, since such information underpins the assessment of ecosystem services provided by green infrastructure, particularly for urban heat island (UHI) mitigation. Although green roofs are widely acknowledged as a promising measure for improving urban thermal comfort, most existing research maps either current green rooftops or rooftops with greening potential, but not both. This study presents a modified deep convolutional neural network rooftop classification framework based on Roofpedia, developed by the Urban Analytics Lab at the National University of Singapore. The proposed model combines high resolution aerial imagery with rooftop slope information derived from a digital surface model and relies entirely on publicly available Swisstopo datasets: SWISSIMAGE orthophotos, swissSURFACE3D elevation data, and swissTLM3D building footprints. Applied to Bern, Switzerland, the model labels rooftops into four categories: existing green roofs, rooftops suitable for green roof installation, rooftops with solar panels, and flat rooftops unsuitable for greening. The framework identifies realistic opportunities for green roof expansion and supplies urban planners with evidence-based information for green infrastructure deployment in Bern and other Swiss cities. Because it is fully open source, the framework is transferable to cities worldwide.

</details>


### [169] [IQ-JEPA: A Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with a Hermitian Vision Transformer for Sound Speed and Attenuation Estimation from Ultrasound IQ Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22351)
*Masashi Sode,Gianmarco Pinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IQ-JEPA，一种基于自监督学习的超声声速重建方法，利用Hermitian视觉Transformer在复IQ信号上学习，提升标签效率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是医学超声成像中的声速重建问题，属于定量超声领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感核心概念完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对自监督学习在信号处理中的通用方法感兴趣，可作为跨领域参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：声速重建（sound speed reconstruction）；场景：医学超声成像（quantitative ultrasound）；传感器：超声探头（2.5 MHz Fullwave仿真）；目标：组织声速图。与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆等）无关。

Model architecture: Hermitian Vision Transformer（Hermitian ViT），直接在复IQ信号上操作，注意力机制具有相位等变性，共轭乘积前馈网络具有相位不变性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。模型为Transformer架构，但未讨论参数量、FLOPs或推理速度。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：79,293个Fullwave 2.5仿真数据（2.5 MHz），其中63,435个无标签，其余有标签；指标：声速误差（m/s）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 在10,000标签下达到15.60 m/s误差；相比监督训练，标签效率提升约3倍（1,000标签下超4倍）；全标签下8.71 m/s；比InversionNet基线差约2.2倍；自监督是主要增益因素；编码器可迁移至不同分布数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨部署完全无关；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未讨论任何轻量化或模型压缩技术；4. 仅在超声仿真数据上验证，未在真实遥感数据上测试；5. 性能低于InversionNet基线。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可能有间接借鉴价值：1. 自监督预训练（如掩码建模）提升标签效率的思路可迁移到遥感目标检测中标签稀缺的场景；2. 利用信号本身的物理不变性（如相位不变性）设计网络结构的思想，可类比到SAR图像的相位信息处理；3. Hermitian ViT对复信号的处理方式可能对SAR复数据或InSAR处理有启发。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: The speed of sound in tissue is a prerequisite for well-focused imaging and has diagnostic value, but recovering it from raw pulse-echo channel data is fundamentally a nonlinear inverse problem. Learned solvers are fast yet label hungry. Simulated sound-speed labels are expensive, while abundant real channel data is unlabeled. We propose IQ-JEPA to exploit both data types. An encoder is pretrained without labels to predict the latent representation of masked in-phase and quadrature (IQ) regions from visible context, then fine-tuned on simulated maps. Sound speed appears in the IQ signal as a phase difference, invariant to the constant phase offset. The encoder is a Hermitian vision transformer that operates on the complex signal directly. Its attention is equivariant to that phase and its conjugate-product feed-forward is invariant to it, so the encoder reads a quantity analogous to the one classical coherence methods use. On 79,293 Fullwave 2.5 simulations at 2.5 MHz, pretraining on the 63,435 unlabeled acquisitions reaches 15.60 m/s at 10,000 labels. This is a roughly threefold gain in label efficiency over supervised training, growing to over fourfold at 1,000 labels. It is about 2.2x below an InversionNet baseline, and 8.71 m/s at full labels. The gain still grows with more unlabeled pretraining data. Our comparisons point to self-supervision as the dominant factor. The same encoder transfers. Its frozen features expose sound speed and attenuation, and cross-distribution pretraining between layered and abdominal phantoms costs little accuracy. We see this as a first step toward a foundation model for quantitative ultrasound.

</details>


### [170] [Integrated Order Dispatching and Routing for Last-Mile Pickup via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22356)
*Yida Xu,Zhaofang Mao,Yuheng Miao,Jiaxin Zhang,Yiting Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个集成优化框架，将学习型路由预测器与实时调度启发式方法结合，解决物流最后一公里取件中的订单调度与路径规划耦合问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于物流最后一公里取件（last-mile pickup）的订单调度与路径规划优化，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：物流订单调度与路径规划（order dispatching and routing）；应用场景：最后一公里取件（last-mile pickup）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Dynamic-Residual Graph Attention Network (GAT) encoder + Look-Ahead Courier-Personalized decoder，用于路径规划子问题；调度子问题使用路由预测器引导的启发式方法（routing-oracle-guided dispatching heuristic with local search）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"real-time scalability"（实时可扩展性），但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：菜鸟物流（Cainiao Logistics）真实世界数据集；评价指标：solution quality（解质量）和 solving time（求解时间）；实验方式：离线评估（offline evaluation）和在线滚动时域仿真（online rolling-horizon simulation）。

Experiments: 在真实物流数据集上，所提方法在解质量和求解时间上均优于其他基准方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，Graph Attention Network作为一种注意力机制在遥感目标检测中也有应用，但本文的GAT用于路径规划而非视觉特征提取，且未涉及轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: In recent years, the growing complexity of last-mile pickup operations has increased the need for fast and accurate decision-making on logistics platforms. This challenge is fundamentally driven by two key and tightly coupled decision-making processes: order dispatching and routing. Solving them separately overlooks their interdependence, while fully end-to-end learning can be unstable and costly on large, variable-scale instances due to sparse rewards. To solve this problem, we propose an integrated optimization framework which couples a learned routing oracle with real-time dispatching heuristics. For the routing subproblem, we develop a Dynamic-Residual Graph Attention Network encoder with a Look-Ahead Courier-Personalized decoder. For the dispatching subproblem, we develop a routing-oracle-guided dispatching heuristic with local search, where the oracle provides near-optimal solutions to select candidate couriers while retaining real-time scalability. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets from Cainiao Logistics are used to test the performance of our approach, including an offline evaluation and an online rolling-horizon simulation. The experimental results show that our approach outperforms other benchmarks regarding solution quality and solving time, indicating it can effectively support logistics companies in solving real-time and large-scale last-mile pickup problems.

</details>


### [171] [Indexing: the Beginning and the End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22361)
*Alexander Kozachinskiy,Vicente Opazo,Felipe Urrutia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"索引原语"任务出发，理论分析RNN、softmax transformer、线性注意力transformer和状态空间模型(SSM)在信息瓶颈下的表达能力，发现低因果复杂度的架构（如低参数RNN、SSM、掩码线性注意力transformer）无法在常数层内解决索引问题，而softmax transformer和未掩码的线性注意力transformer则具备更强表达能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等工程方法。论文是纯理论性的深度学习表达能力分析，属于机器学习/理论计算机科学范畴。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：理论分析深度学习架构的表达能力（索引原语任务）。应用场景：无。传感器类型：无。目标类别：无。

Model architecture: RNN、softmax transformer、线性注意力transformer、状态空间模型(SSM)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。虽然文中提到"low-parameter RNNs"，但这是理论分析中的参数规模限制，并非模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 合成数据：索引原语任务，n bits + 1个索引，n最大为64。指标：学习成功率（是否能学会该任务）。

Experiments: 实验针对n≤64的索引任务进行，结果与理论分析定性一致：具有低参数理论解的配置容易学习，而不具备理论解的配置随着序列长度增长难以学习。

Limitations: 1. 纯合成任务，与真实遥感/视觉任务无直接关联；2. 实验规模极小（n≤64），无法反映大规模遥感场景；3. 未涉及任何视觉任务或遥感目标检测；4. 未提供任何轻量化部署相关的工程指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的间接启发：论文对不同架构（RNN、SSM、transformer变体）的表达能力进行了理论对比，如果课题中考虑在轨部署时选择轻量序列模型（如Mamba等SSM架构），可以关注其表达能力边界。但论文本身不提供任何遥感场景下的实用指导。

Abstract: We study information bottlenecks in modern deep-learning architectures -- RNNs, softmax transformers, linear-attention transformers and state-space models -- through the lens of the indexing primitive. In this primitive, the input consists of $n$ bits and one integer $i$ from $1$ to $n$ called the index, and the output equals the value of the $i$-th bit.
  We introduce causal complexity for masked architectures. We show that architectures with low causal complexity cannot solve the indexing primitive in any constant number of layers when the index appears at the end of the input. In particular, this limitation applies to low-parameter RNNs, SSMs and masked linear-attention transformers. In contrast, small softmax transformers can solve it in one layer, while non-masked linear-attention transformers can solve it in 2, which separates them from their masked counterparts. In turn, when the index appears at the beginning, we show that small RNNs are capable of solving this task in 1 layer, while all the other architectures require 2.
  All our impossibility results are unconditional and apply even to models that employ infinite-precision real arithmetic. Moreover, experiments for up to $n=64$ qualitatively align with our theory: configurations with low-parameter theoretical solutions learn the indexing task easily, while configurations that do not admit such theoretical solutions struggle to learn as the sequence length grows.

</details>


### [172] [Interior interpretability with attention rollout: contraction and propagation profiles in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22367)
*Umberto Biccari,Qian Huang,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"内部可解释性"视角，利用注意力展开（attention rollout）和Doeblin-Dobrushin收缩理论分析表格型Transformer的中间层传播特性，用于代谢组学年龄预测任务。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于Transformer的可解释性理论分析，应用场景为代谢组学年龄预测（tabular data），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：Transformer内部可解释性分析；应用场景：代谢组学年龄预测（非遥感/卫星场景）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 表格型Transformer（Tabular Transformer），使用attention rollout作为传播分析工具。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 代谢组学年龄预测数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；使用Dobrushin系数、rollout传播轮廓等可解释性指标。

Experiments: 在代谢组学年龄预测任务上，测量了rollout收缩随深度增强的趋势；与PCA和GradientExplainer近似SHAP进行了比较，发现高排名变量局部一致但完整排名弱一致。

Limitations: 1. 论文明确说明attention rollout仅作为传播诊断工具，不作为因果解释或完整归因；2. 实验未建立个体rollout排名变量的预测相关性；3. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下两点可间接参考：1. 如果未来在遥感Transformer中做可解释性分析，attention rollout结合Dobrushin系数可作为分析模型内部传播特性的工具；2. 对Transformer内部传播的收缩性分析可能对理解轻量化后模型的信息流变化有启发，但需要自行迁移到遥感场景。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods assign scores relating input variables to a model's output, but do not by themselves characterize how explicitly defined interaction operators compose across its intermediate layers. We introduce \emph{interior interpretability}, a propagation-based perspective on internal model organization, and instantiate it for tabular Transformers using attention rollout. We interpret rollout as a row-stochastic operator encoding attention-mediated propagation between feature tokens. By applying classical Doeblin--Dobrushin contraction theory, we show that a rollout operator with a small Dobrushin coefficient is quantitatively close to a rank-one stochastic matrix whose common row is determined by its normalized column sums. This result gives a structural interpretation to the corresponding rollout propagation profile. In Transformers trained for metabolomic age prediction, the measured rollout contraction strengthens with depth. Trained and randomly initialized models also exhibit different propagation profiles, although the present experiments do not establish the predictive relevance of individual rollout-ranked variables. Exploratory comparisons with PCA and GradientExplainer approximations to SHAP reveal localized agreement among highly ranked variables but weak agreement across complete rankings. Attention rollout is therefore used here as a diagnostic of attention-mediated propagation, not as a causal explanation or faithful attribution of the complete Transformer.

</details>


### [173] [LunarFM: A Shared Multimodal Representation of the Moon's Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22408)
*Marc Girona-Mata,Jakob Gawlikowski,Sumit Goski,Gautier Bardi de Fourtou,Valentin T. Bickel,Ben Moseley,Abigail Calzada-Diaz,Sylvester Kaczmarek,Raúl Ramos-Pollán*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LunarFM是一个面向月球表面的多模态基础模型，利用多任务多传感器轨道遥感数据学习通用表征，支持相似性搜索、少样本资源映射、矿物丰度回归和地质单元分类等下游任务。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。论文涉及遥感（月球轨道遥感）、多模态数据融合和基础模型，但与"目标检测"无关（不涉及目标检测任务），与"轻量化/在轨部署"完全无关（未讨论模型压缩、推理加速或星上部署）。仅遥感场景有间接参考价值。

Reading priority: 1分 — 与课题方向弱相关。不涉及目标检测，不涉及轻量化/在轨部署，仅遥感场景有间接参考价值，阅读优先级低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态表征学习、相似性搜索、少样本分类、回归（矿物丰度）、地质单元分类。场景：月球表面遥感。传感器类型：6种仪器（来自3个月球任务），18个输入通道。目标类别：月球表面地质单元、矿物丰度等，不涉及目标检测中的物体类别（舰船/飞机/车辆等）。

Model architecture: 多模态掩码自编码器（Multimodal Masked Autoencoder），将多传感器多通道数据映射到768维共享嵌入空间。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节（如Transformer/CNN设计、编码器-解码器配置等）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。模型为768维嵌入的大规模基础模型，从描述看参数量可能较大，不具备在轨部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自构建的多模态月球表面数据集，覆盖纬度70°S-70°N，18通道共配准观测数据。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标（如分类准确率、回归RMSE等）。提供预训练模型和嵌入数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。仅列举了支持的下游任务类型（相似性搜索、少样本资源映射、矿物丰度回归、地质单元分类）。

Limitations: 1. 与目标检测任务完全无关，不涉及任何目标定位/识别；2. 无任何轻量化或部署相关讨论；3. 模型规模未知，但基础模型通常参数量大，不适合星上部署；4. 仅覆盖月球中低纬度（70°S-70°N），极区未覆盖；5. 未报告定量实验结果，缺乏性能对比。

Ideas for my research: 1. 多模态融合思路：可借鉴其多传感器多通道数据映射到共享嵌入空间的方法，用于遥感卫星多模态数据（光学+SAR+高光谱）的联合表征学习；2. 少样本学习策略：其少样本资源映射方法可迁移到遥感小样本目标检测场景；3. 但该论文与"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的核心方向（检测+轻量化）匹配度低，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: The renewed global focus on lunar exploration, driven by the prospect of in-situ resource utilization and a sustained human presence on the Moon, has created growing demand for accurate, large-scale characterization of the lunar surface. Although vast quantities of orbital remote-sensing data have been collected, scientific analysis and resource mapping remain fragmented by heterogeneous multiinstrument observations, sparse labels, and bespoke task-specific modelling workflows. Here we introduce LunarFM, a multimodal foundation model that learns a general representation of the lunar surface from diverse orbital measurements. LunarFM assimilates observations from six instruments across three lunar missions, mapping 18 input channels to a shared embedding space. We demonstrate that this embedding space supports a diverse range of downstream applications, including similarity search, few-shot resource mapping, mineral abundance regression, and geological unit classification, enabling efficient scientific investigation and resource-oriented analysis. We provide a machine-learning-ready dataset of co-registered multimodal observations spanning latitudes from 70°S to 70°N, a pretrained multimodal masked autoencoder, and a companion embedding dataset providing a joint 768-dimensional representation of lunar surface properties. All code and data are available at https://lunarfm.trillium.tech/

</details>


### [174] [On the Identifiability of Controlled World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22430)
*Xiangteng Zhang,Yang Guan,Bo Zhang,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究世界模型（world model）中联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在可控环境下的状态与动态可识别性理论，属于无模型/基于模型的强化学习与表征学习理论工作。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘部署等模型压缩方法。论文属于表征学习/世界模型/控制理论方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及遥感场景或目标检测任务。任务类型为世界模型学习与可控环境下的状态可识别性理论分析。

Model architecture: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs)，用于在表征空间中学习世界模型。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。实验在非线性观测映射和行为策略下进行，用于验证可识别性理论。

Experiments: 实验在非线性观测映射和行为策略下进行，验证了所提出的可识别性理论，并展示了在transition可识别性、反事实预测和以目标为条件的潜在规划方面的意义。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 论文本身是理论性工作，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或模型轻量化，因此对在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题无直接参考价值。理论假设（高斯潜在状态、高斯行为策略）在实际遥感部署场景中可能不成立。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。如果强行关联，JEPA框架中在表征空间进行预测的思想，或许可以启发遥感目标检测中的特征级知识蒸馏方法（在隐空间对齐教师和学生模型），但论文本身并未涉及蒸馏或轻量化，且理论假设与遥感场景差异较大。

Abstract: Learning world models that infer environment dynamics from high-dimensional observations and predict outcomes under candidate actions is central to planning and control. Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provide a compelling framework for learning such models in representation space. Recent action-conditioned extensions perform promisingly in visual control and latent-space planning, but leave a fundamental question unresolved: when does controlled latent prediction identify both the underlying state and the controlled dynamics? This is challenging under nonlinear observations and behavior policies with limited conditional action variation, where state-dependent evolution and action effects can be statistically confounded. We establish a joint identifiability theory for controlled world models with Gaussian latent states under state-dependent Gaussian behavior policies. We identify two policy-dependent conditions: spectral separation of the predictable signal governs representation identifiability, while non-degenerate conditional action variation governs transition identifiability. We prove that when both conditions hold, every global minimizer of the JEPA objective identifies the latent state and controlled transition up to an orthogonal transformation. We further derive quantitative bounds on representation and transition identifiability under approximate optimization. Finally, we construct predictor perturbations along weakly excited action directions whose counterfactual-to-on-policy error ratio is the inverse transition-identifiability margin, revealing the cost of limited action coverage. Experiments across nonlinear observation maps and behavior policies corroborate the theory and demonstrate implications for transition identifiability, counterfactual prediction, and goal-conditioned latent planning.

</details>


### [175] [Hyperball May Not Be a Free Lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22444)
*Yihao Xiao,Jialong Sun,Zitian Gao,Zeming Wei,Chutian Wang,Ran Tao,Jiaye Teng,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Hyperball风格优化器（如MuonH、MuonWD）在大规模训练中的优势来源，发现其性能差异主要来自有效步长演化而非更新方向，并指出学习率调度对发挥其潜力至关重要。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于深度学习优化器（Hyperball-style optimizers）的理论分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感/卫星场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。不涉及目标检测模型或遥感网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。仅提及"pretraining experiments"，未给出具体数据集名称或评价指标。

Experiments: 在训练配置下进行了数值实验，对比了MuonH和MuonWD优化器的收敛行为，通过启发式实验修改学习率调度使优化器动力学相互复现，并进行了预训练实验验证学习率衰减策略的影响。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身是优化器理论分析，不涉及任何应用场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然本文不直接涉及遥感或轻量化，但其对优化器（尤其是学习率调度策略）的分析思路可间接启发：在遥感在轨目标检测模型训练中，若使用类似Hyperball风格的优化器，需注意学习率调度对收敛速度和最终性能的影响。但整体借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: For scale-invariant deep networks, Hyperball-style optimizers have shown strong performance in large-scale training by fixing the norms of matrix-valued parameters and normalizing updates. However, the source of their advantage remains unclear. Starting from the angular displacement between consecutive parameter states, we derive an angular effective learning rate that accounts for the parameter-update angle, parameter norm, and update norm. We also show that the conventional norm-based measure is a special case under parameter-update orthogonality. We then decompose optimizer updates into radial and tangential components and analyze how radial updates affect one-step angular displacement. Under the training configurations considered, numerical results show that the radial component has only a limited direct effect on the angular effective learning rate. It therefore cannot explain why MuonH converges more slowly than MuonWD early in training but overtakes it later. To further isolate the underlying mechanism, we devise a heuristic experiment that modifies only the learning-rate schedule so that the dynamics of each optimizer reproduce those of the other. The results suggest that their main difference stems from the evolution of the effective step size rather than an intrinsically superior update direction induced by Hyperball. Our pretraining experiments further show that more aggressive learning-rate decay can accelerate MuonH early in training but may impair its later performance. Thus, maintaining a constant angular velocity does not eliminate the learning-rate-scheduling problem; careful scheduling remains essential to realizing the potential of Hyperball-style optimizers. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mangocrazz/hyperball-may-not-be-a-free-lunch.

</details>


### [176] [Phylogenetic signal in marine mammal and bird vocalizations captured by audio foundation models: the limited benefit of domain-specific pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22458)
*Víctor Rincón Yepes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究预训练音频模型（AST、CLAP、BEATs-bio、BirdNET）的嵌入空间是否隐式编码了系统发育距离（物种进化关系），发现通用音频基础模型能恢复强系统发育信号，且领域专用预训练并非必要条件。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是音频/声学信号（marine mammal vocalizations, bird songs），任务为系统发育距离恢复（phylogenetic distance recovery），属于计算生物声学与表征学习领域。完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：系统发育信号探测（phylogenetic signal probing）；场景：生物声学（marine mammals, birds）；传感器/数据：音频录音（Watkins Marine Mammal Sound Database, bird recordings）；目标类别：32种海洋哺乳动物（含26种鲸类）、20种鸟类。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: AST (Audio Spectrogram Transformer)、CLAP (Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining)、BEATs-bio (bioacoustic variant of BEATs)、BirdNET（端到端分类器，训练于约6000种鸟类）。均为音频预训练模型，与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Watkins Marine Mammal Sound Database（1754条录音，32种海洋哺乳动物）；鸟类录音（20种，基于Jetz et al. 2012系统发育树）。评价指标：Mantel test相关系数r（Mantel r）、partial Mantel r。不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测指标（mAP, F1等）。

Experiments: 主要结果：鲸类中CLAP r=0.82, BEATs-bio r=0.82, AST r=0.74（均p<0.001）；手工MFCC特征r=0.040（p=0.338）；PCA降维至105维后信号仍存在；控制主频的partial Mantel r=0.404。鸟类实验中AST r=0.55, CLAP r=0.52, BirdNET和BEATs-bio约0.32-0.36。无遥感相关实验。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法评估其局限性对遥感目标检测的参考价值。摘要本身未讨论模型效率、推理速度等。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于生物声学与表征学习交叉领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无任何技术交集。不推荐借鉴。

Abstract: Do learned audio embeddings encode structure that nobody told them to encode? We probe four large pretrained audio models (AST, CLAP, BEATs-bio and BirdNET) with a downstream task none of them saw during training: recovering phylogenetic distance from species vocalizations. If the geometry of the embedding space tracks the tree of life, the representation is picking up something deeper than the labels the model was optimized for.
  We run Mantel tests across two independent radiations. In 32 marine mammal species (1,754 recordings from the Watkins Marine Mammal Sound Database) the foundation models recover strong phylogenetic signal within the 26 cetaceans (CLAP r=0.82, BEATs-bio r=0.82, AST r=0.74; all p<0.001), among the highest acoustic-phylogenetic correlations reported for any taxon. Hand-crafted MFCC features (105d) find nothing (r=0.040, p=0.338). The gap survives after PCA-projecting every embedding down to 105 dimensions, so it is not an artefact of representation size. It also survives a partial Mantel test controlling for dominant frequency (partial Mantel r=0.404, keeping 97% of the variance explained), so it is not just pitch in disguise.
  We repeat the analysis on 20 bird species using the Jetz et al. (2012) phylogeny, and this time add BirdNET, a classifier trained end-to-end on around 6,000 bird species. The general-purpose foundation models recover the signal again (AST r=0.55, CLAP r=0.52). The unexpected result is that neither BirdNET nor the bioacoustic BEATs-bio beat them (r around 0.32 to 0.36). Matching the training domain to the target taxon does not, by itself, help. Pretrained audio embeddings carry evolutionary information across two independent radiations, and domain-specific pretraining is not required for it to emerge.

</details>


### [177] [Complexity Bounds and Approaches to Learning Projected Gradient Descent Solver Iterates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22467)
*Anjian Li,Ryne Beeson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何通过k邻域数据收集策略增强生成模型的训练数据，以解决参数优化问题中数据稀缺的挑战，并给出了基于Rademacher复杂度的泛化界分析。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接或间接的借鉴。论文聚焦于优化求解过程中的数据增强理论，应用场景完全不同。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究方向与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"完全无关，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署中的任何关键问题。

Abstract: Data scarcity poses a fundamental challenge in training generative models to produce initial guesses for parametric optimization problems that are otherwise numerically expensive to solve. We therefore study a $k$-neighborhood data collection strategy that augments datasets of converged solutions with intermediate solver iterates, increasing the amount of training data without additional solver runs. To understand the benefits of this approach, we derive a generalization bound based on Rademacher complexity that reveals the role of the $k$-neighborhoods and related parameters. To achieve this result, we focus on one-sided box-constrained quadratic programs solved by projected gradient descent. We illustrate the behavior of this solver on two examples. The approach proposed in this paper enables a more capable DDDAS paradigm by improving the efficiency of the data-model-optimization loop. We finish by discussing two views of learning solver-iterate data and connect our analysis with GLENS, a new data-efficient global search method.

</details>


### [178] [\k{appa}-LoRA: Condition Numbers Reveal Which LoRA Matrices Worth Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22489)
*Jianghui Wang,Silong Yong,Francesco Orabona,Marco Canini,Katia P. Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出κ-LoRA，通过条件数（condition number）筛选出最值得更新的LoRA矩阵（仅更新top 50%），在保持精度的同时将可训练参数量减半，微调时间减少16.2%，显存降低4.5%。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。该论文属于大模型高效微调（LoRA）方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测无直接关系。虽然提到了"edge deployment"和"on-device fine-tuning"，但核心场景是通用大模型微调，并非遥感在轨目标检测。轻量化思路（条件数驱动的选择性更新）有一定借鉴意义，但整体方向偏离。

Reading priority: 2分 — 阅读优先级低。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，仅条件数驱动的选择性更新思路有微弱借鉴意义。如果课题涉及星上模型微调/增量学习，可快速浏览；如果聚焦推理阶段轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏），则无需阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型/大模型的参数高效微调（Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, PEFT）。应用场景：通用大模型微调，提及边缘部署和端侧微调但未具体展开。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于LoRA的低秩适配结构，核心创新在于通过条件数（condition number，即最大奇异值与最小奇异值之比）评估每个LoRA矩阵的重要性，仅更新条件数最大的top 50%矩阵。模型主体为通用Transformer/大模型架构，未指定具体模型名称。

Lightweight method: 提出κ-LoRA，一种基于条件数排序的选择性LoRA更新策略。核心轻量化手段：① 计算每个LoRA矩阵的条件数；② 仅更新条件数最大的前50%矩阵；③ 其余50%矩阵冻结不更新。该方法将可训练参数量减半，降低计算和显存开销。属于参数高效微调（PEFT）范畴，不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明具体部署硬件或平台。仅提及：可训练参数量减半（top 50%矩阵更新），微调时间减少16.2%，显存降低4.5%。未提供参数量绝对值、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等具体部署指标。提及"edge deployment"和"on-device fine-tuning"但无量化数据支撑。与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）无直接关联。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：微调精度（accuracy，与标准LoRA持平）、微调时间（减少16.2%）、显存成本（降低4.5%）、可训练参数量（减半）。实验在"multiple benchmarks"上进行，但未列出具体benchmark名称。

Experiments: 主要实验结果：κ-LoRA（top 50%条件数最大的矩阵）与标准LoRA精度持平，可训练参数量减半，微调时间平均减少16.2%，显存降低4.5%。进一步分析发现：被选中的矩阵的条件数在训练过程中持续下降，表明κ-LoRA的有效性来源于"目标性谱再平衡"（targeted spectral rebalancing）而非单纯的参数选择。未提供与剪枝、量化等其他轻量化方法的对比。

Limitations: ① 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于通用大模型微调方法；② 仅验证了精度持平，未说明在资源极度受限场景（如星上）的实际部署效果；③ 条件数计算本身引入额外开销，摘要未分析这部分代价；④ 仅针对微调阶段，不涉及推理阶段的轻量化；⑤ 实验仅在通用NLP/多模态benchmark上，未在遥感视觉任务上验证。

Ideas for my research: ① 条件数（condition number）作为重要性评估指标的思想可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的轻量化中，例如用于评估检测头中不同通道/层的重要性，指导结构化剪枝或通道选择；② 选择性更新/选择性激活的思路可用于遥感在轨模型的增量学习或域自适应微调场景，在星上有限算力下只更新最关键参数；③ 谱分析（spectral analysis）方法可尝试用于分析遥感目标检测模型的特征表示质量；④ 但需注意该方法面向的是微调阶段而非推理阶段，与"在轨推理轻量化"的核心需求不完全匹配。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely adopted technique for efficient neural network fine-tuning, decomposing model updates into low-rank matrices. However, LoRA remains computationally costly because it updates all matrices uniformly, regardless of their actual contribution to adaptation. This cost is especially prohibitive for large-scale models with billions of parameters and for resource-constrained settings such as edge deployment and on-device fine-tuning. We show for the first time that not all LoRA matrices are equally worth tuning: matrices with smaller condition numbers (the ratio of largest to smallest singular value) are already well-balanced across directions and contribute only marginally to adaptation, whereas matrices with larger condition numbers contain underdeveloped directions that span richer subspaces and drive most of the performance gains. This observation itself is a key contribution of our work, and it motivates a more selective approach to fine-tuning. Building on this insight, we propose \k{appa}-LoRA, a method that optimizes LoRA by focusing updates on the matrices with the largest condition numbers, which capture the most informative directions of change. By restricting LoRA updates to the top 50% of weight matrices ranked by condition number, \k{appa}-LoRA halves the trainable parameter count and correspondingly reduces compute and memory cost. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that this design cuts fine-tuning time by 16.2% on average while matching the accuracy of standard LoRA and reducing memory cost by 4.5%. Further analysis reveals that the condition numbers of the selected matrices consistently decrease over training, suggesting that \k{appa}-LoRA's effectiveness stems from targeted spectral rebalancing rather than parameter selection alone.

</details>


### [179] [Susceptible Reservoir Architectures for Regime-Conditional Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22491)
*Aliaksei Kaliutau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于储层计算（Reservoir Computing）的波动率预测架构SUSA，用于金融时间序列预测，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究金融波动率预测（volatility forecasting），涉及股票和ETF时间序列分析。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等任何检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等任何轻量化或部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：金融波动率预测（volatility forecasting）。应用场景：美国股票和ETF市场。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: SUSA（Susceptible Architectures）储层计算架构，包括复值开链和周期储层（complex-valued open-chain and periodic reservoirs），以及基于状态条件专家模型（regime-conditioned experts）的储层特征解释模块。还实现了开放系统q-qubit量子版本（Qiskit）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。该论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。该论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：16个美国股票和ETF时间序列（U.S. equity and exchange-traded-fund series）。评价指标：QLIKE损失函数。实验设置：三个不相交的时间序列训练/验证/测试划分，12个观测值的输入窗口，5个观测值的预测范围。

Experiments: 与GARCH模型对比，在特定资产（IWM, XLP）上取得统计显著的QLIKE改进。与HARQ-style预测互补，堆叠集成（stacked ensemble）使平均QLIKE提升0.0116，在75%测试场景中获胜。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。该论文聚焦金融时间序列预测，其储层计算方法虽可视为一种轻量计算范式，但论文未讨论任何与遥感图像目标检测或星上部署相关的内容。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。储层计算（Reservoir Computing）作为一种轻量级递归神经网络范式，理论上可被探索用于遥感时序数据处理（如变化检测），但本论文未涉及任何遥感或目标检测内容，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Volatility forecasting is dominated by persistence and measurement noise, leaving limited residual structure for nonlinear models to exploit. We introduce Susceptible Architectures (SUSA), a reservoir-design principle for volatility forecasting, and its two concrete implementations, based on complex-valued open-chain and periodic reservoirs and regime-conditioned experts to interpret reservoir features across calm, onset, recovery, and persistent-stress states. We also implement open-system $q$-qubit counterparts in Qiskit while retaining a common AR-Ridge anchor and a bounded residual correction trained under QLIKE. We evaluate models on 16 U.S. equity and exchange-traded-fund series using three disjoint chronological training, validation, and test folds, a 12-observation input window, and a five-observation forecast horizon. The proposed models perform competitively with GARCH, achieving statistically significant QLIKE improvements for specific assets (IWM, XLP). Also models' forecasts complement HARQ-style predictions: a stacked ensemble improves mean QLIKE by 0.0116 over its strongest constituent and wins in 75% of test scenarios.

</details>


### [180] [Dysphagia Risk Stratification in Head and Neck Cancer via Two-Stage PRO-Clinical Stacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22514)
*Siyuan Zhao,Eric Ababio Anyimadu,Zachary G. Brumm,Yue Ma,Clifton David Fuller,Xinhua Zhang,G. Elisabeta Marai,Guadalupe Canahuate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于患者报告结局(PRO)和临床变量的两阶段堆叠模型，用于预测头颈癌治疗后吞咽障碍风险，无需视频荧光成像。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学影像（视频荧光吞咽造影）和临床预测模型，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：吞咽障碍风险预测；场景：头颈癌幸存者护理；传感器类型：视频荧光成像（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。摘要未说明遥感相关任务。

Model architecture: 两阶段堆叠模型（two-stage stacking model），具体结构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该模型面向临床决策支持，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 使用MDADI（MD Anderson Dysphagia Inventory）患者报告数据及结构化临床变量。具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要提到MDADI个体响应比复合/全局评分包含更多预测信息，可解释性分析揭示了与吞咽障碍相关的症状模式和临床风险因素。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医学临床预测建模领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Dysphagia is a debilitating late effect of head and neck cancer (HNC) treatment, yet timely identification of at-risk patients remains challenging in survivorship care. Definitive assessment relies on videofluoroscopic imaging, as captured by the Dynamic Imaging Grade of Swallowing Toxicity (CTCAE-DIGEST), which, while validated, requires specialized equipment, trained personnel, and significant patient burden, limiting its routine use in surveillance. Patient-reported outcomes (PROs), by contrast, are low-cost, scalable, and easily collected at any clinical encounter, making them an attractive alternative signal for identifying patients who may warrant further evaluation. However, a clear clinical framework for translating PRO responses into actionable interventions is still evolving. In particular, uncertainty remains regarding when a patient's self-reported symptom burden should prompt escalation of care.
  This study addresses this gap by formulating a single-visit PRO-clinical prediction framework and introducing a clinically interpretable two-stage stacking model to predict swallowing impairment risk using PRO responses and structured clinical variables, without requiring videofluoroscopic imaging. The proposed framework quantifies the independent contributions of patient-reported symptoms and clinical factors within a unified and interpretable risk assessment model. Our findings demonstrate that individual MDADI responses contain predictive information beyond that captured by composite or global summary scores, while interpretability analyses reveal symptom patterns and clinical risk factors associated with swallowing impairment. Together, these results support the use of structured PRO-clinical integration as a practical, imaging-free approach for dysphagia risk stratification in HNC survivorship.

</details>

### [181] [Deep Sigma Point Processes for RCS Modeling in Spaceborne SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21745)
*Khalid El-Darymli,Christoph H. Gierull,Katerina Biron,Weimin Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出深度西格玛点过程(DSPP)模型用于SAR图像中舰船RCS预测，通过贝叶斯高斯过程框架量化预测不确定性，在RADARSAT-2数据集上相比线性回归基线降低20.83%的RMSE。

Research relevance: 1分。虽然论文涉及SAR遥感（RADARSAT-2数据集、舰船目标），但其核心任务是RCS建模与预测，而非目标检测；且完全不涉及任何轻量化、模型压缩、在轨部署或实时推理相关内容。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。该论文聚焦RCS概率建模而非目标检测，且未涉及任何轻量化或部署技术。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：RCS回归预测（非目标检测）；应用场景：SAR图像舰船RCS建模；传感器类型：SAR（RADARSAT-2）；目标类别：舰船（208,191个验证样本）。

Model architecture: 深度西格玛点过程(DSPP)模型，基于层次化高斯过程框架，采用Matern核函数与自动相关性确定(ARD)机制，结合贝叶斯推断进行不确定性量化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。高斯过程模型通常计算复杂度较高(O(n³))，不利于星上部署，但摘要未讨论此问题。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：RADARSAT-2 SAR数据集，包含208,191个验证舰船样本；评价指标：RMSE（降低20.83%）、R-squared（提升25.89%）、残差四分位距(IQR)和中位绝对偏差(MAD)（均降低44.4%）。

Experiments: 与线性回归基线对比，DSPP在RMSE、R-squared、残差IQR和MAD四个指标上均显著优于基线。摘要未说明与其他先进方法的对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 高斯过程模型计算复杂度高，难以满足在轨实时推理需求；2. 仅做RCS回归预测，不涉及目标检测任务；3. 未讨论模型参数量、推理速度等部署关键指标；4. 仅在单一数据集(RADARSAT-2)上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的是：1) 使用Matern核+ARD进行特征重要性排序的思路，可用于分析遥感目标检测中哪些特征对检测性能贡献最大，辅助剪枝或特征选择；2) 不确定性量化方法可迁移到在轨检测中，用于评估检测结果的置信度。但整体与轻量化目标检测方向关联很弱。

Abstract: Radar cross-section (RCS) modeling is foundational to advancing the utility and sensitivity of spaceborne radar systems. This study introduces a deep sigma-point process (DSPP) model for predicting RCS in synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery using a RADARSAT-2 dataset containing 208,191 verified ships. The DSPP model not only strives for predictive accuracy but also characterizes the uncertainty inherent in the intricate relationships among radar signals, ship parameters, and environmental conditions. Unlike traditional approaches that rely on deterministic equations with static parameters, the DSPP uses a hierarchical Gaussian process framework with Bayesian inference to capture variability and uncertainty in RCS predictions. By generating predictive distributions rather than single estimates, the model accounts for the complex dynamics governing radar returns. Using a Matern kernel with automatic relevance determination, the DSPP identifies and ranks critical features across radar, operational, and environmental domains, thereby supporting transparency and interpretability. Performance evaluations demonstrate the model's superiority over linear regression baselines, with a 20.83 percent reduction in root mean squared error, a 25.89 percent increase in R-squared, and a 44.4 percent reduction in both the residual interquartile range and median absolute deviation on the test data. By providing calibrated uncertainty bounds, the DSPP enhances prediction reliability and supports robust decision-making. This work represents a shift toward probabilistic models that incorporate the inherent uncertainty of complex phenomena. By transitioning from fixed equations to distributions over outcomes, the DSPP fosters a deeper understanding of RCS behavior and enables systems to operate effectively in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [182] [Leveraging Agonistic-Antagonistic Coactivation in Single-Grid HDsEMG for Hand Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21809)
*Firas Darwish,Dhiyaa Al Jorf,Costanza Armanini,Eion Tyacke,Farah E. Shamout*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文通过利用拮抗肌群冗余性，证明仅使用伸肌网格的sEMG信号即可达到与双网格系统相当的HGR手势识别精度，从而将硬件和计算需求减半。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是表面肌电信号(sEMG)手势识别，属于人机交互/生物医学工程领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感内容，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然论文讨论了计算复杂度和硬件需求降低，但这是通过数据源简化（减少传感器通道）实现的，而非模型压缩或加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：Hand Gesture Recognition (HGR) 手势识别；应用场景：人机交互、假肢控制；传感器类型：高密度表面肌电信号(sEMG)；目标类别：16种手势。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: CNN架构，具体包括单网格CNN（仅伸肌或仅屈肌）和双网格CNN（联合训练），以及slow joint fusion变体。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文的"轻量化"思路是通过减少输入传感器通道（从双网格减为单网格）来降低计算和硬件需求，而非剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到计算复杂度和硬件需求可减半，但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：高密度sEMG信号数据集，20名受试者，16种手势；评价指标：balanced accuracy（平衡准确率）、AUROC；实验结果：伸肌单网格89.5% balanced accuracy / 0.99 AUROC，双网格94.6% / 1.00 AUROC。

Experiments: 对比了单网格CNN（伸肌/屈肌分别训练）与双网格CNN（联合训练），以及slow joint fusion变体。消融实验表明伸肌单网格性能接近双网格，slow joint fusion未带来提升。GradCAM可视化与解剖分析表明伸肌信号质量优于屈肌。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 仅针对基础DoF手势集，未说明对复杂手势的泛化性；3. 未涉及任何模型轻量化技术；4. 无参数量、FLOPs等量化指标；5. 未讨论边缘/嵌入式部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的思路是：在遥感场景中，是否某些光谱波段或极化通道存在冗余，可以通过减少输入通道数来降低计算量。但这种思路属于数据层面的简化，而非模型轻量化本身，且遥感领域已有大量波段选择工作。

Abstract: Surface Electromyography (sEMG) is critical for intention prediction in human-computer interfaces, such as for prosthetics control. Although deep learning models for Hand Gesture Recognition (HGR) yield excellent results, they impose high computational and hardware demands. This paper addresses this bottleneck by exploiting redundancies in agonist-antagonist muscle activity, hypothesizing that coactivations present in the sEMG signals from the extensor or flexor groups alone are sufficient for accurate HGR. We evaluate this by comparing convolutional neural networks (CNNs) trained on one muscle grid against CNN architectures trained jointly on both grids. Experiments were conducted using 16 gestures from a dataset of high-density sEMG signals from 20 subjects. The results demonstrate that the extensor grid alone achieves performance (89.5% balanced accuracy, 0.99 AUROC) comparable to the dual-grid system (94.6% balanced accuracy, 1.00 AUROC). Notably, even when applying slow joint fusion to capture spatial features across grids, model performance did not improve. GradCAM visualizations and anatomical analysis further indicate that the extensor region provides superior signal quality compared to the flexors. Our findings suggest that for a base set of DoF gestures, HGR hardware requirements and computational complexity can be halved without a prohibitive loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [183] [Advances in Wavelet Denoising for Communication Signals: From Parameter Selection Toward Data-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21825)
*Priyalakshmi Sheela,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 一篇关于小波去噪在通信信号（OFDM）中参数选择策略的综述与基准测试论文，提出了统一决策框架，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是通信信号（OFDM）的小波去噪参数选择问题，属于通信信号处理领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及目标检测或任何视觉任务。虽然提到了FPGA和DSP约束，但这是通信基带处理的硬件部署，与星上遥感目标检测模型的轻量化部署无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通信信号去噪与小波参数选择；应用场景：OFDM通信系统、IEEE 802.11n信道估计；传感器类型：通信信号（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: DWT（离散小波变换）、SWT（平稳小波变换）、WPT（小波包变换）；Unified Decision Framework（统一决策框架）；提及可扩展至deep-unfolding架构。不涉及任何目标检测模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。文中提到的"real-time feasibility"和"embedded DSP/FPGA constraints"属于通信信号处理的实时实现约束，并非模型压缩/加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到embedded DSP/FPGA constraints和real-time feasibility，但这是针对通信信号处理算法的硬件部署，而非遥感目标检测模型的星上部署。未提供参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等与模型轻量化相关的指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成pilot-aided OFDM信道估计数据、实测IEEE 802.11n信道数据；评价指标：SNR gain、MSE、BER、EVM、NMSE、Friedman检验、Wilcoxon检验、efficiency-index ranking。均为通信信号处理指标，与遥感目标检测的mAP、F1、IoU等无关。

Experiments: 对DWT、SWT、WPT在OFDM去噪下进行基准测试；提出的Unified Decision Framework在合成数据和实测IEEE 802.11n数据上显著优于固定参数小波和经典基线（p<10^{-11}），具有最低估计误差并泛化到未见数据。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题完全无关。该论文解决的是通信信号处理中的小波参数选择问题，不涉及任何视觉检测任务或遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然小波去噪在遥感图像预处理中可能有应用，但该论文聚焦于通信信号而非图像，且不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。如果未来研究方向涉及遥感图像的小波域去噪/增强作为检测前处理，可参考其参数选择方法论，但与"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"的核心课题关联极弱。

Abstract: Wavelet denoising suppresses nonstationary, impulsive, and interference-like disturbances in communication signals, but its effectiveness depends on jointly selecting the transform family, mother wavelet, decomposition level, thresholding rule, and shrinkage function. This review synthesises studies published during 2020--2025 across ten sources using a PRISMA-aligned protocol and classifies them by parameter-selection focus and application domain. The evidence shows a shift from fixed empirical choices toward similarity-, sparsity-, entropy-, energy-, sub-band-SNR-, and task-loss-driven selection, while revealing limited communication-specific validation. To address this gap, DWT, SWT, and WPT are benchmarked for OFDM denoising under impulsive noise using SNR gain, MSE, BER, EVM, real-time feasibility, Friedman and Wilcoxon tests, efficiency-index ranking, and embedded DSP/FPGA constraints. Results show that improved waveform fidelity does not necessarily translate into better hard-decision performance, motivating receiver-level validation. A Unified Decision Framework is therefore developed and validated on synthetic pilot-aided OFDM channel estimation and measured IEEE 802.11n channels using BER, EVM, NMSE, and SNR gain. The selected configuration significantly outperforms fixed-parameter wavelet and classical baselines $\left(p < 10^{-11}\right)$, achieves the lowest estimation error, generalises to held-out data, adapts to channel conditions, and supports extension to deep-unfolding architectures.

</details>


### [184] [Noncoherent Detection and Interference Nulling for Terrestrial-Satellite Downlinz Coexistence in the Upper Mid-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21838)
*Shizhen Jia,C. Nicolas Barati,Marco Mezzavilla,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究地面基站与卫星上行链路共存时的非相干感知与干扰零陷波束设计，不涉及遥感目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦地面-卫星通信共存中的干扰管理（interference nulling, beamforming），属于无线通信物理层领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：非相干受害者感知与干扰零陷；场景：地面基站与卫星上行链路共存（upper mid-band）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明深度学习模型架构。涉及GLRT（广义似然比检验）、MDL（最小描述长度）、MUSIC算法等传统信号处理方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用site-specific ray-tracing仿真数据；评价指标：NTN INR（非地面网络干扰噪声比）降低、TN SINR（地面网络信干噪比）退化程度。

Experiments: 通过射线追踪仿真验证，表明所提方法能显著降低NTN INR，同时仅对TN SINR造成适度退化。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩或部署方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于通信干扰管理方向，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Terrestrial--satellite coexistence in the upper mid-band is challenging when a terrestrial base station has limited prior information about non-terrestrial receivers and their uplink transmissions. This paper studies noncoherent victim sensing and interference nulling, where uplink sensing snapshots are observed while the transmitted waveform is treated as unknown. For a single victim, we show that the generalized likelihood-ratio test reduces to a principal-eigenvector estimator of the sample covariance. For multiple victims, we combine MDL-based model-order selection with MUSIC to recover anonymous direction and power information that is sufficient for beam design without pilot knowledge or user identities. These estimates are then used in a nulling beamformer that preserves the intended terrestrial link while reducing leakage toward detected victims. We further analyze the single-victim estimator in the large-matrix regime and show that estimation accuracy improves with sensing SNR, which reveals the observed interplay between path loss and estimation quality. Site-specific ray-tracing results show significant reduction of NTN INR with only modest degradation of TN SINR.

</details>


### [185] [Baseband-Free Wideband MU-MIMO OFDM via Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21900)
*Zheao Li,Jiancheng An,Chau Yuen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于级联堆叠智能超表面(SIM)的无基带宽带MU-MIMO OFDM发射机架构，将符号加载、预编码和OFDM调制从数字基带迁移到波域实现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于无线通信物理层/超表面天线设计领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然摘要中出现了"quantization-aware training"（量化感知训练），但这里的量化是指超表面离散相位约束下的优化问题，与深度学习模型量化压缩无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无线通信发射机架构设计；应用场景：MU-MIMO OFDM宽带通信；传感器类型：堆叠智能超表面(SIM)；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 两级级联SIM架构：SIM1负责符号加载和信道自适应MU-MIMO预编码，SIM2负责波域OFDM调制（IDFT+CP插入）。属于电磁/通信硬件架构设计，非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"quantization-aware training (QAT)"是指针对超表面离散相位约束的优化方法，并非深度学习模型压缩中的量化技术。不涉及剪枝、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该架构面向基站侧宽带通信，与星上部署无关。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel baseband-free wideband MU-MIMO OFDM transmitter architecture enabled by two cascaded stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIMs). Unlike conventional wireless transmitters, the proposed design shifts symbol loading, MU-MIMO precoding, and OFDM modulation from digital baseband to the wave domain. Specifically, in the first SIM (SIM$_1$), a programmable symbol-loading interface first loads one stacked virtual-subcarrier coefficient vector onto a common monochromatic carrier, while the remaining layers perform channel-adaptive MU-MIMO precoding from the data streams to the port-subcarrier domain. Then, the second SIM (SIM$_2$) realizes offline-configured wave-domain OFDM modulation by implementing the inverse discrete Fourier transform (IDFT) and cyclic-prefix (CP) insertion operator directly in the wave domain. Based on this architecture, we develop a wave-domain system model that explicitly characterizes the virtual-to-physical transition from block-level coefficients to radiated OFDM subcarriers. The designs of SIM$_1$ and SIM$_2$ are formulated as two operator-fitting problems, respectively targeting a block-diagonal wideband precoder and the ideal OFDM modulation operator. Under practical discrete phase constraints, we develop a quantization-aware training-based gradient descent (QAT-GD) framework for both SIM stages. Numerical results verify the effectiveness of the proposed optimization, the correctness of the synthesized wave-domain functionalities, and the strong end-to-end MU-MIMO OFDM performance of the resulting architecture.

</details>

Abstract: Establishing and maintaining 5G mmWave vehicular connectivity poses a challenge due to high user mobility, requiring the design of robust and efficient beam switching procedures. Unlike reactive beam switching based on channel state information (CSI) feedback received from vehicular users, proactive beam switching exploits CSI prediction to prepare in advance for upcoming beam switching decisions. In this paper, we develop a framework for autonomous and self-trainable CSI prediction for mmWave vehicular users. In the proposed framework, base stations (gNBs) collect and label data sets to train a CSI prediction model both independently and using federated learning (FL). The data set combines data extracted from the CSI feedback and cellular vehicle-to-everything (C-V2X) cooperative awareness messages (CAMs) of surrounding vehicles. The framework is placed in the context of machine learning and artificial intelligence (ML/AI)-based Open RAN (O-RAN) applications (rApps and xApps) fed by realistic real-world mobility and CSI data from the DeepMIMO simulator. Detailed evaluation results demonstrate feasibility, accuracy, and flexibility of the proposed CSI prediction framework

</details>

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出螺旋磁共振波谱成像(MRSI)中时间交错采样伪影的数学描述与后处理校正方法，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医学成像信号处理领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Purpose: In Spiral Magnetic Resonance Spectroscopic Imaging (MRSI), achieving suitable spatio-temporal resolutions requires interleaving. Sampling inconsistencies occurring between temporally interleaved signals, -whether due to time-interleaved ADCs or phase/frequency mismatches in the excitation and demodulation stages-, result in artifacts in the recombined data. This paper proposes a mathematical description of these unavoidable artifacts and a postprocessing solution to circumvent the issues and mitigate them.
  Methods: The study proposed a detailed description and explanation of these artifacts using data acquired with MRSI interleaved sequence with gradients off, as well as standard interleaved FID sequence. The random signal mismatch between the interleaves was described as additional artifactual signals $ψ$(t) at each temporal interleave. A model was further proposed to match the acquired signal in relation to the ideal, artifact-free signal. Considering M interleaves, a correction method was derived relying on estimating 4 parameters in the $ψ$(t) model for each interleave by minimizing a multivariable cost function. This correction method was evaluated on spectra acquired from phantoms and in vivo acquisitions obtained in healthy volunteers.
  Results: The proposed correction method significantly reduced artifacts by a factor of 4 in the phantom and 1.8 in the healthy volunteers. This correction ensured accurate spectral quantification in regions where artifacts overlapped with the content of interest, such as lipids in subcutaneous fat. Additionally, it was demonstrated that appropriately selecting the number of temporal interleaves can shift the artifact away from the frequency of interest, leveraging the periodic and limited frequency span of the observed artifacts.
  Conclusion: In spiral-MRSI, temporal interleaves, while enhancing spectral bandwidth, introduced spurious content characterized by distinctive resonance frequencies and nonreproducible amplitudes and phases. The study proposes two solutions: manipulating the number of interleaves to control artifact frequency localization or using a retrospective correction method to approximate and attenuate artifacts.

</details>


### [188] [Noise-Robust Frequency Estimation via Overlapped Sampling-Intervals Zero-Crossing Fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22096)

Experiments: 在SNR=10dB条件下，OS-ZFM相比传统过零点拟合法中位数估计误差降低60%以上，相比基本过零点检测降低90%，且优于基于Hilbert变换的估计器。实验验证使用激光多普勒干涉测量的冲击诱导瞬态运动数据。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。摘要未说明该方法在图像/视觉任务上的适用性，也未涉及任何深度学习模型压缩或加速技术。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于信号处理领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交集。

Abstract: The trade-off between noise averaging and temporal resolution fundamentally limits conventional zero-crossing frequency estimators under dynamic and noisy conditions. This paper presents an overlapped sampling-intervals zero-crossing fitting method (OS-ZFM), which introduces a structured overlapping regression framework that decouples noise averaging from temporal update rate. The method adopts a deterministic closed-form formulation, enabling a unified bias-variance analysis to characterize the statistical behavior of the estimator and clarify the role of structured data reuse. Numerical results under intensity and background noise at a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of 10 dB show that OS-ZFM reduces median estimation error by more than 60\% compared to conventional zero-crossing fitting methods at the same temporal resolution. It further achieves up to 90\% reduction relative to basic zero-crossing detection and consistently yields lower estimation errors than Hilbert-transform-based estimators. Experimental validation utilizing impact-induced transient motion measured by laser Doppler interferometry demonstrates that OS-ZFM reconstructs smooth and physically consistent trajectories with improved temporal fidelity. Owing to its low computational complexity and deterministic formulation, the proposed method enables accurate real-time frequency and acceleration tracking in resource-constrained measurement systems.

</details>

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文讨论的是通信系统的计算复杂度，与遥感卫星在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：仿真数据（300 GHz, N=1024, 16-QAM）；评价指标：BER（误码率）、CRB（Cramér-Rao界）、SNR范围10-45 dB。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 仿真实验表明CPTR在SNR=10-45 dB范围内与CRB差距在0.2 dB以内，复杂度低于Kalman方法，BER优于CPE、线性插值和三次样条方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于通信信号处理领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) communication systems for sixth-generation (6G) networks are severely impaired by Wiener phase noise (WPN), whose innovation variance at sub-THz carriers is substantially larger than in millimeter-wave 5G systems. Conventional common-phase-error (CPE) compensation applies a single phase rotation per OFDM symbol and becomes inadequate when the phase trajectory varies significantly within the symbol duration. This letter proposes continuous phase trajectory reconstruction (CPTR), a closed-form intra-symbol phase noise tracking method that reconstructs the sample-level phase trajectory from pilot observations via least-squares polynomial fitting with $\mathcal{O}(N_p+N)$ complexity. We characterize the polynomial approximation error under WPN and derive the Cramér--Rao bound (CRB) for polynomial phase coefficient estimation, showing that CPTR is minimum-variance unbiased within the polynomial surrogate model. Simulations at 300~GHz with \textit{N}~=~1024 and 16-QAM show that CPTR remains within 0.2~dB of the CRB across SNR~=~10--45~dB while achieving significantly lower complexity than Kalman-based tracking and substantial BER gains over CPE, linear interpolation, and cubic spline methods.

</details>


### [190] [Innovation-Domain Decision-Directed Phase Tracking for Wiener Phase Noise in Fast Rayleigh Fading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22131)
*Ura Klongklaew,Nithiroth Pornsuwancharoen,Phichai Youplao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种创新域决策导向相位跟踪架构，用于瑞利衰落信道下的相干检测，核心是相位跟踪与低复杂度滤波。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全属于通信信号处理领域，研究的是瑞利衰落信道下的相位跟踪与相干检测问题，与遥感、卫星在轨目标检测、模型轻量化没有任何关联。既不是遥感/卫星场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及轻量化模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息。文中仅提到O(1) per-symbol complexity，与遥感模型部署的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标无关。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于通信物理层信号处理，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究范畴完全不同。

Abstract: This letter proposes an innovation-domain decision-directed phase tracking (ID-DDPT) architecture for coherent detection over Rayleigh fading channels with temporally correlated phase evolution and Wiener phase noise. By reformulating phase tracking into the innovation domain, replacing the unbounded cumulative phase with its stationary increments, the proposed method converts a non-stationary estimation problem into a stable low-complexity filtering problem. A closed-form expression for the steady-state residual phase error variance is derived under the locked-regime assumption, along with an analytical optimal smoothing factor. Modeling the residual phase distortion as an effective signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) attenuation yields a tractable bit error rate (BER) approximation for BPSK over Rayleigh fading. A first-order error-propagation analysis further characterizes the impact of decision errors and provides insight into the onset of cycle slips. Simulation results demonstrate that ID-DDPT outperforms DBPSK and a complexity-equivalent scalar Kalman tracker (SKT), achieving near-coherent performance with $\mathcal{O}(1)$ per-symbol complexity and minimal pilot overhead.

</details>

Datasets and metrics: 使用仿真数据进行评估，评价指标包括曲率提取精度（curvature extraction accuracy）、认证精度（authentication accuracy）和欺骗成功概率（spoofing success probability）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何可借鉴点。建议忽略此论文。

Abstract: This letter exploits near-field (NF) spherical wavefronts to derive geometry-dependent physical signatures for backscatter device (BD) authentication in extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) backscatter communication systems. We propose a binary element-wise delay shift keying (EW-DSK) modulation scheme based on a multi-antenna BD architecture. The proposed scheme introduces a deterministic, bit-dependent delay that shifts the power delay profile (PDP) while preserving the aperture-dependent curvature induced by NF propagation. Using the dominant PDP component extracted across the receive antennas, we develop a compact delay-curvature signature extractor and a corresponding authentication test to mitigate BD impersonation attacks. Simulation results evaluate the proposed scheme in terms of curvature extraction accuracy, authentication accuracy, and spoofing success probability under different spoofer distances, signal-to-noise ratios, and effective bandwidths.

</details>


### [192] [Temperature-aware Optimization of Liquid Crystal Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces: Physics-based Modeling and Robust Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22141)
*Mohamadreza Delbari,Bowu Wang,Arash Asadi,Vahid Jamali*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究LC-RIS（液晶可重构智能表面）辅助无线通信系统中的温度自适应安全传输问题，提出不依赖完整CSI的相位偏移设计方法，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是一种基于SDP（半定规划）的优化方法作为基准，以及一种低复杂度启发式方法用于相位偏移设计，并非深度学习模型架构。
