Abstract: While LC technology facilitates the realization of energy-efficient and scalable RISs, their phase shift response is inherently temperature-dependent. Neglecting this thermal dependency can lead to performance degradation, which is particularly detrimental in secure wireless systems where phase-shift inaccuracies may result in unintended information leakage. To address this challenge, we investigate secure communication in LC-RIS-aided systems and develop a temperature-adaptive phase-shift design. Beyond thermal sensitivity, the massive number of elements at mmWave frequencies is required to compensate for high path loss. This large-scale deployment of LC-RISs can lead to significant overhead challenges due to the acquisition of CSI. To ensure practical feasibility, this work proposes a phase-shift design that does not rely on the full CSI; instead, it employs only the possible locations of legitimate users and potential eavesdroppers. By illuminating a spatial zone rather than a single target location, the proposed temperature-adaptive algorithm enhances robustness against both thermally induced phase errors and positioning inaccuracies. To solve the resulting optimization problem, we present an SDP-based approach to serve as a high-performance benchmark, as well as a low-complexity heuristic method. The latter demonstrates superior scalability as the number of RIS elements increases, which makes it highly effective for deploying extremely large surfaces in dynamic, real-time environments. Based on this scalable framework, we further design a temperature-robust algorithm that maintains high security without requiring real-time temperature data. Extensive simulation results confirm that our temperature-adaptive and temperature-robust approaches yield a superior secrecy rate compared to conventional designs that neglect temperature impacts.

</details>


### [193] [Neuromorphic Non-Orthogonal Multiple Access for Parallel Remote Inference via Vector Symbolic Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22155)
*Jiechen Chen,Zihang Song,Dengyu Wu,Bipin Rajendran,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非正交多址接入（NOMA）和向量符号架构（VSA）的神经形态通信协议NOMA-NC，用于多设备并行远程推理，利用脉冲神经网络（SNN）的稀疏表示实现节能通信与计算。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于边缘智能系统中的多设备并行远程推理通信协议，使用神经形态传感和SNN，不涉及遥感/卫星场景、目标检测任务，也未讨论模型轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）。虽然涉及SNN的稀疏计算和节能，但应用场景和任务类型与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不推荐优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多设备并行远程分类推理（parallel remote inference）；应用场景：边缘智能系统、多传感器网络；传感器类型：事件驱动神经形态传感器（event-driven neuromorphic sensing）；目标类别：N-MNIST手写数字、DVS128 Gesture手势识别；不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测。

Model architecture: 核心框架：NOMA-NC协议，基于向量符号架构（VSA）实现非正交多址接入（NOMA）神经形态通信；编码端：每个设备将稀疏脉冲特征图与设备特定置换键绑定（binding）；信道：空中叠加直接实现VSA的捆绑（bundling）操作；解码端：共享解码SNN + 轻量级每设备学习解绑定（learned unbinding），单次推理恢复所有决策。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确讨论传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。但SNN本身的动态稀疏表示（dynamically sparse representations）天然具有计算和通信节能特性，可视为一种"事件驱动"的轻量化思路。轻量级每设备解绑定模块（lightweight per-device learned unbinding）提及了轻量设计，但未给出具体参数量或FLOPs。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提及接收端计算能量节省与同时活跃设备数量呈亚线性关系（sub-proportional），且不增加每设备传输能量。未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：N-MNIST（神经形态手写数字）、DVS128 Gesture（动态视觉传感器手势识别）；评价指标：goodput（有效吞吐量）、接收端计算能量节省；未涉及遥感数据集或目标检测指标（mAP、F1等）。

Experiments: 实验结果表明NOMA-NC在goodput增益和接收端计算能量节省方面优于先前方法，且节省程度与同时活跃设备数量呈亚线性关系。未提供与遥感目标检测方法的对比。

Limitations: 1. 仅验证了简单分类任务（N-MNIST、DVS128 Gesture），未在遥感目标检测场景下验证；2. 未讨论模型参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等关键轻量化指标；3. 未涉及遥感图像（光学/SAR/高光谱）或目标检测任务；4. 通信协议设计面向多设备并行分类，与单星/多星在轨检测场景差异大。

Ideas for my research: 1. SNN的事件驱动稀疏计算思路可借鉴：遥感在轨场景中，若采用事件相机或对变化区域进行稀疏处理，可降低计算和传输能耗；2. VSA框架的捆绑/解绑操作实现多路信号叠加解码的思路，对多星协同检测或星上多任务融合可能有启发；3. 但整体而言，该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点有限，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Emerging edge intelligence systems increasingly rely on dense deployments of always-on sensors that must convey task-relevant information to a remote model under tight energy and spectral budgets. The deployment of event-driven neuromorphic sensing paired with spiking neural networks (SNNs) is attractive in this regime because it produces dynamically sparse representations, so that energy is spent on communication and computation only when informative events occur. Prior multiple-access protocols for remote inference using neuromorphic sensing and computing targeted collaborative settings, in which the server fuses information from all devices into a single decision. This paper instead addresses parallel remote inference, in which each device observes a distinct input, and requires its own classification decision. We propose NOMA-NC, a non-orthogonal multiple-access (NOMA) neuromorphic communication (NC) protocol built on the vector symbolic architecture (VSA) framework. In NOMA-NC, each device binds its sparse spike feature map with a device-specific permutation key, and all devices in a group transmit concurrently so that the over-the-air superposition directly realizes the VSA bundling operation. A shared decoding SNN, together with lightweight per-device learned unbinding, recovers all decisions in a single inference pass. Experiments on the N-MNIST and DVS128 Gesture datasets show that NOMA-NC yields goodput gains and savings in terms of receiver computing energy that are sub-proportional to the number of simultaneously active devices, without increasing the per-device transmission energy.

</details>


### [194] [Group-Connected Riemannian Manifold Optimization for Reciprocal BD-RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22170)
*Marko Fidanovski,Thushar Venkataramanaiah,Eduard Jorswieck,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu*

Main category: eess.SP

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) provide a flexible means of engineering wireless propagation channels through configurable multiport scattering networks. Among their architectures, group-connected beyond-diagonal RISs (BD-RISs) offer a practical tradeoff between scattering flexibility and hardware complexity by partitioning the surface into inter-connected groups. In reciprocal and lossless implementations, each group scattering block must be symmetric and unitary. In this paper, we investigate sum-rate maximization for reciprocal group-connected BD-RIS-assisted multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems under such structural constraints. In particular, we build on a recent result where the feasible space determined by the symmetry and unitary constraints was characterized as a Riemannian manifold, extending it into a product manifold of symmetric-unitary block manifolds, thus expanding the approach to manifold phase optimization in the group-connected setting. As a consequence, the proposed group-extended framework performs tangent-space projections and Takagi retractions block-wise, while preserving the coupling among all groups through the common MIMO equivalent channel. The formulation naturally includes single-connected and fully connected reciprocal BD-RIS architectures as special cases and enables a flexible performance-complexity tradeoff through the group size.

</details>


### [195] [Propagation models for IEEE 802.15.6 standardization of implant communication in body area networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22197)

Abstract: A body area network is a radio communication protocol for short-range, low-power, and highly reliable wireless communication for use on the surface, inside, or in the peripheral proximity of the human body. Combined with various biomedical sensors, BANs enable realtime collection and monitoring of physiological signals. Therefore, it is regarded as an important technology for the treatment and prevention of chronic diseases, and health monitoring of the elderly. The IEEE 802 LAN/MAN Standards Committee approved Task Group TG15.6 in December 2007. As a result of more than four years of effort, in February 2012, TG15.6 published the first international standard for BANs, IEEE Std 802.15.6. Throughout the development of this standard, ample collaboration between the standardization group and the research community was required. In particular, understanding the radio propagation mechanisms for BANs demanded the most research effort. Technical challenges were magnified for the case of implant communication because of the impossibility of conducting in-body measurements with human subjects. Therefore, research in this field had to make use of intricate computer simulations. This article outlines some of the research that has been done to obtain accurate propagation models supporting the standardization of implant communication in BANs. Current research to enhance the channel models of IEEE Std 802.15.6 through the use of ultra wideband signals for implantable devices along with physical measurements in animals is also presented.

</details>


### [196] [A Foundation Model for Cross-Band CSI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22204)
*Hongpu Zhang,Shu Sun,Ruifeng Gao,Tongjia Zhang,Feng Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种跨频段CSI重建的基础模型，利用功率-角度-延迟谱和导频引导的交叉注意力机制，在多个无线信道数据集上取得显著性能提升。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信领域的跨频段信道状态信息（CSI）重建问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然使用了encoder-decoder架构和cross-attention等通用技术，但应用场景、数据模态和任务目标与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无直接关联。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：跨频段CSI重建（cross-band CSI reconstruction）；场景：多频段低空无线系统（multi-band low-altitude wireless systems）；传感器类型：无线信道测量；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 基础模型（foundation model），采用encoder-decoder架构，使用功率-角度-延迟谱（power-angle-delay spectrum）作为公共表示，射频元数据（RF metadata）作为辅助条件输入，引入pilot-guided cross-attention融合源频段结构与目标频段导频信息。

Experiments: 在三个数据集上，模型相比最优的pair-specific基线平均降低NMSE 6.1 dB；在未见过的两个频段对上无需配对微调即可迁移，相比全监督基线分别获得7.5 dB和7.1 dB增益；在目标导频含噪情况下仍保持有效。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向所需的目标检测、遥感图像处理、模型压缩或边缘部署方面的信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文中的pilot-guided cross-attention和foundation model思想属于通信领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题不相关。

Abstract: Acquiring dense high-frequency channel state information (CSI) is costly in multi-band low-altitude wireless systems because pilot resources are limited and channel dimensions change with the carrier frequency, bandwidth, and antenna array size. We address cross-band CSI reconstruction, which recovers dense target-band CSI from dense source-band CSI and sparse, noisy target-band pilots. We propose a foundation model that represents every band in a common power-angle-delay spectrum and uses radio-frequency metadata as auxiliary conditioning for an encoder-decoder. The model uses pilot-guided cross-attention to fuse source-band structure with target-band pilot, allowing one model to handle heterogeneous band pairs. It is trained by pilot-densification pretraining followed by supervised cross-band fine-tuning. On ray-tracing, 3GPP, and DeepMIMO datasets, the model lowers average normalized mean-square error by 6.1 dB over the state-of-the-art pair-specific baseline. It also transfers to two unseen pairs without paired fine-tuning, achieving 7.5 and 7.1 dB gains over fully supervised baselines, and remains effective when target pilots are noisy.

</details>

Model architecture: 统计信道模型，基于异构解剖模型和频率依赖的人体组织介电特性进行数值仿真。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用异构解剖模型进行数值仿真（非公开遥感数据集）；评价指标：路径损耗（dB）、均方根时延扩展（ns）。

Experiments: 分析了20 mm和80 mm两种植入深度，平均路径损耗分别约为20 dB和50 dB，均方根时延扩展均低于1 ns。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无。该论文属于生物医学通信领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉点。

Abstract: Implant medical wireless sensors for monitoring physiological parameters, automatic drug provision, and so on represent a new promising healthcare technology. Inherent characteristics of ultra-wideband (UWB) radio make this technology highly suitable for the wireless interface of implant sensors. A communication channel model is essential for developing these wireless systems. However, there are currently few models describing the radio propagation inside the human body. To address this problem, a statistical model is presented for UWB propagation channels inside the human chest in the 1-6 GHz frequency range. The proposed statistical model is developed from numerical simulations using a heterogeneous anatomical model that includes the frequency-dependent dielectric properties of different human tissues. Mathematical formulas for the computation of path loss, scattering and the statistical implementation of the channel impulse response at different depths inside the chest are described. Two typical depths for implanted sensors in the chest, namely 20 and 80 mm are analysed in detail. Average path loss of approximately 20 and 50 dB is observed in each case, respectively. Moreover, the channel exhibits little time dispersion with a root-mean-square delay spread below 1 ns in both cases. These results aim at facilitating the tasks associated with the design of in-body medical communication systems.

</details>


### [198] [Depthwise Separable CNN for D-MIMO Indoor Localization with Data Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22217)
*Georgios Mystriotis,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Achiel Colpaert,Sofie Pollin*

TL;DR: 提出一种面向D-MIMO室内定位的轻量级分布式ML框架，通过在边缘部署轻量模型（1.39 MB内存、1.96 MFLOPs）处理CSI特征提取，将回传流量降低100倍，同时保持亚厘米级定位精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦室内定位（indoor localization）和D-MIMO/O-RAN通信架构，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然论文提出了轻量化方法（1.39 MB内存、1.96 MFLOPs）和边缘部署策略，但应用场景和任务类型与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：室内定位（indoor localization）；场景：D-MIMO/O-RAN通信网络；传感器：CSI（信道状态信息）信号，非遥感传感器；目标类别：无目标检测任务，仅做位置估计。

Model architecture: 分布式ML框架：边缘侧（DU）部署轻量模型进行CSI特征提取与降维，中心侧（CU）部署另一模型进行位置估计。具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 轻量级分布式ML框架，将初始处理迁移至网络边缘。边缘模型仅需1.39 MB内存和1.96 MFLOPs。通过边缘特征提取与降维减少回传数据量。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 边缘模型参数量/内存：1.39 MB；计算量：1.96 MFLOPs；回传流量降低100倍。但部署平台为O-RAN架构的DU/CU，非星上/边缘嵌入式平台（如FPGA/NPU/TensorRT），与在轨部署场景差异较大。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：高密度室内定位数据集（摘要未说明具体名称）；指标：平均定位误差8.5 mm；回传流量压缩比100x。

Experiments: 在减少一半RU（Radio Units）的条件下，仍保持8.5 mm平均误差，回传流量降低100倍。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 应用场景为室内定位，与遥感目标检测任务完全不相关；2. 未涉及遥感图像/信号处理；3. 未涉及目标检测的任何技术要素（anchor、NMS、bbox回归、分类等）；4. 轻量化指标（1.96 MFLOPs）虽低，但任务复杂度远低于遥感目标检测，直接借鉴意义有限。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1）边缘-中心协同的分布式推理架构思想，类似星上边缘处理+地面中心融合的思路；2）轻量模型部署的具体指标（1.39 MB、1.96 MFLOPs）可作为星上部署资源约束的参考下限。但整体而言，该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Indoor localization using Distributed Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (D-MIMO) and machine learning (ML) achieves sub-centimeter accuracy but faces midhaul capacity bottlenecks when transmitting raw Channel State Information (CSI) in Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) architectures. To address this, we propose a lightweight, distributed ML framework that shifts initial processing to the network edge. By deploying localized models as dApps on Distributed Units (DUs), each requiring just 1.39 MB of memory and 1.96 MFLOPs, the system performs CSI feature extraction and reduction on the edge. The reduced low-dimensional features are transmitted to the Central Unit (CU), where another dApp is deployed for location estimation. Evaluated on a high-density dataset, this framework reduces midhaul traffic by 100x while maintaining an average error of 8.5 mm, even with half the deployed Radio Units (RUs), providing a scalable blueprint for practical D-MIMO localization.

</details>


### [199] [Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Resilient Distributed Radar Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22357)
*Christian Eckrich,Maik Pfefferkorn,Rolf Findeisen,Abdelhak M. Zoubir,Vahid Jamali*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究移动机器人/车辆系统中分布式FMCW雷达融合的量化-延迟权衡，推导CRLB以分析信道容量约束下的目标状态估计不确定性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于地面移动机器人/车辆的分布式雷达感知融合，不涉及遥感卫星、光学/SAR卫星图像、在轨目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及雷达和量化，但场景是地面短距FMCW雷达的通信-感知权衡，而非遥感卫星对地观测。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Lightweight method: 涉及量化（quantization），但这是通信层面的量化（rate-distortion theory），而非模型轻量化中的权重量化或激活量化。不涉及剪枝、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。论文讨论的是通信信道容量约束下的量化与更新率权衡，而非模型在边缘/星上的部署问题。

Datasets and metrics: 使用数值仿真（numerical simulations），未使用遥感标准数据集。评价指标为CRLB（Cramer-Rao lower bound）和不确定性度量。

Experiments: 通过数值仿真展示了量化 fidelity 与更新率之间的 tradeoff，以及系统参数选择对不确定性降低的影响。未与目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何深度学习目标检测模型；3. 不涉及模型轻量化或边缘部署；4. 场景为地面短距雷达，非星载SAR/光学遥感；5. 仅做理论推导和数值仿真，缺乏真实数据验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：1. 量化与延迟的权衡思想（rate-distortion vs. information aging）在星地链路通信约束下有一定启发，但需转换到深度学习模型压缩场景；2. Fisher信息矩阵融合的思路可用于多星协同感知的不确定性分析，但与本课题核心方向距离较远。

Abstract: Distributed radar sensing enables safe and resilient operation in mobile robotic and vehicular systems by combining multiple local views of the scene. In this paper, we consider a moving agent equipped with a frequency modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar that fuses its local measurements with those of surrounding static radar sensors to reduce the uncertainty in target state estimation. The benefit of sharing measurements over capacity-limited wireless links come at the expense of two competing degradation mechanisms: quantization distortion, which increases the effective noise floor, and latency, which causes the received information to age. A unified framework is thus needed to quantify how individual radars contribute to uncertainty reduction under limited communication resources. To this end, we derive the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) for the fused target state by combining local Fisher information matrices (FIMs) through coordinate transformations, incorporating distortion bounds from rate-distortion theory and information aging via a state transition model. We show that the resulting expression reveals a fundamental tradeoff between update rate and quantization fidelity governed by the available channel capacity. Our numerical simulations illustrate this tradeoff and demonstrate that a careful choice of system parameters (e.g., selected radar systems, quantization, update rate) is necessary for maximum uncertainty reduction.

</details>

### [200] [Prior laundering: learned priors with inherited, undetectable overconfidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21721)
*Ali Siahkoohi,Sina Alemohammad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文讨论基于生成先验的贝叶斯反问题中"先验洗白"导致的过度自信问题，与遥感目标检测和轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。核心是贝叶斯反问题中的不确定性量化与先验偏差问题，属于计算成像/地球物理/医学成像领域的理论分析工作。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：贝叶斯逆问题求解（Bayesian inverse problems）；应用场景：地震成像（seismic imaging）、医学成像（medical imaging）、地下水反演（groundwater operator）；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及了扩散先验（diffusion prior）和归一化流（normalizing flow）作为生成先验的代表。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等内容。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。实验涉及合成数据或真实地震/医学数据，但未给出名称。

Experiments: 通过理论分析和实验（扩散先验在地震成像、归一化流在地下水反演）展示了先验洗白导致的覆盖不足问题。但实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 论文本身讨论的是贝叶斯反问题中先验洗白的局限性，而非遥感目标检测的局限性。与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供直接参考。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，几乎无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果在轨遥感目标检测中使用贝叶斯不确定性估计（如MC Dropout、Bayesian CNN），需注意先验偏差问题，但本文讨论的场景（地震/医学成像反问题）与遥感目标检测差异极大。

Abstract: Learned generative priors are increasingly used for ill-posed Bayesian inverse problems, their posterior uncertainty treated as earned from data. But training one requires truths, scarce in seismic and medical imaging, so the recourse is an archive of legacy reconstructions---prior laundering. Where the measurements are uninformative the posterior reverts to the prior, so the uncertainty reported there is the archive's, not the data's, and nothing in deployment reveals it: truths differing only on those directions induce identical data laws, and self-consistency checks such as simulation-based calibration pass whatever the prior believes. That belief has an exact source: averaging the legacy posterior over the measurements yields the old regularizer advanced a single expectation--maximization step---improved where the data resolve, frozen where they cannot. It is overconfident wherever the inherited belief is tighter than the truth. A single-best archive is worse, collapsing the blind credible interval to zero width. Deployed, a diffusion prior fit to the archive under-covers the operator's blind subspace, unlike a truth-trained control, and a normalizing flow does the same on a nonlinear groundwater operator. We recommend reporting which directions the measurements resolve, separating confidence the data support from belief inherited through the pipeline.

</details>


### [201] [Simulation-Based Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21843)
*Xinwei Shen,Diana Cai,Cheng Zhang,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模拟的隐式似然经验贝叶斯方法（SBEB），用于在似然函数不可解析计算时进行经验贝叶斯推断。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于贝叶斯统计推断与模拟推断（simulation-based inference）领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个主题。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) performs simultaneous inference across many related latent variables. Classical EB assumes that the likelihood p(x | z) is tractable. In many scientific applications, however, the likelihood is available only through a simulator. This paper develops EB for such implicit likelihoods. We introduce simulation-based empirical Bayes (SBEB), which connects nonparametric EB to simulation-based inference (SBI). SBEB computes EB estimates without an explicit density by using the observed data, simulator samples, and an amortized inference network. SBEB iteratively refines the fitted EB prior toward the population prior. With several scientific simulators and real-world data, we demonstrate that SBEB improves accuracy over SBI with a fixed prior.

</details>


### [202] [Efficient Online LLM Watermark Detection via Rao-Blackwellized E-Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21958)
*Lu Luo,Dandan Mo,Chengdong Xu,Ting Li,Jinhan Xie,Huiqiong Li,Niansheng Tang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Rao-Blackwellized e-processes的在线水印检测框架，用于区分AI生成文本与人类撰写文本，支持流式生成中的任意停止验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM生成文本的水印检测（NLP领域），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明目标检测模型结构。提出的是基于Rao-Blackwellized e-processes的统计检测框架，非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。虽提及"efficient"，但指的是统计检测效率而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明遥感数据集。实验使用"real LLM-generated text"（真实LLM生成文本），评价指标为Type I error控制、asymptotic log-growth等统计指标。

Experiments: 通过仿真和真实LLM文本实验验证了在线检测的有效性和anytime-valid保证。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP领域的统计水印检测，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed, reliable and efficient mechanisms for distinguishing AI-generated text from human-written content have become essential. Statistical watermarking has emerged as a promising solution, yet most existing methods are typically fixed-horizon procedures, precluding valid early stopping in streaming generation. In this paper, we develop an efficient online watermark detection framework with anytime-valid inference based on Rao-Blackwellized e-processes, enabling recursive token-level evidence updates without storing the full history. In particular, we instantiate the framework for the Gumbel-max watermark and reduce the original token-level dependence testing problem to a pivot-induced sequential testing problem with an explicit null distribution. Theoretically, we prove anytime-valid Type I error control under arbitrary optional stopping and establish positive asymptotic log-growth under watermarking, implying consistency of the proposed stopping rules. Simulations and experiments on real LLM-generated text demonstrate efficient online detection with rigorous anytime-valid guarantees.

</details>


### [203] [Convergence analysis of a family of Zermelo-type iterations for the Bradley--Terry model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22221)
*Ruijian Han,Ding Lu,Yiming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究Bradley-Terry模型中Zermelo型迭代算法的局部收敛性分析，提出异步更新下α=0为最优参数选择，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。本文研究的是Bradley-Terry统计模型中的参数估计算法收敛性分析，属于统计学习和优化理论范畴。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及任何遥感或计算机视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及任何深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要提及在合成数据和真实数据集上进行了数值实验，但未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要提及通过数值实验验证了理论分析结果，但未给出具体实验对比数据。

Limitations: 本文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文属于统计优化理论，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何关联。

Abstract: Zermelo's algorithm is a classical method for computing the maximum likelihood estimator in the Bradley--Terry (BT) model, but its convergence can be slow in practice. To accelerate computation, Newman introduced a family of Zermelo-type fixed-point iterations parameterized by $α$, with Zermelo's algorithm recovered at $α=1$. Empirical evidence suggests that the choice $α=0$ often converges substantially faster, making it a promising alternative, yet the mechanism underlying this acceleration remains elusive. This paper provides theoretical insight into this phenomenon through a systematic local convergence analysis. We derive closed-form expressions for local convergence factors under synchronous and asynchronous updates and analyze their dependence on $α$ via spectral analysis of the associated Jacobian matrices. For synchronous updates, we show that the algorithm may fail to converge when $α<1$, and its local convergence factor is quasi-convex in $α$ under the population BT model. In contrast, asynchronous updates are always locally convergent, and their local convergence factor is provably monotonically increasing in $α$ under the population BT model of consistently ordered bipartite comparison graphs, establishing the optimality of $α=0$ in this setting. We further establish asymptotic approximation results for the population convergence factors under the BT model, justifying their practical relevance. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm the theory. Our analysis complements existing convergence results and shows that the acceleration of $α=0$ arises not only from the parameter choice but, more importantly, from the use of asynchronous updates.

</details>


### [204] [Variational Low-rank Tensor Decomposition for Multisubject Spatiotemporal Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22262)
*Laura M. Montaldo,Ricardo A. Borsoi,Sebastian Miron,Tulay Adali*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种时空变分张量分解（ST-VTD）框架，用于多被试神经影像数据的共享与个体特异性结构建模，结合LL1分解和LSTM先验，通过展开优化算法进行变分推断。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于神经影像（fMRI）多被试时空数据的张量分解方法，属于计算神经科学/脑成像分析领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或任何轻量化部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对张量分解在遥感数据压缩中的泛化应用有特别兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多被试时空数据分解（张量分解）；应用场景：神经影像分析（fMRI）；传感器类型：功能磁共振成像（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: ST-VTD框架：结合张量分解生成模型与结构化先验；空间因子采用LL1分解启发的低秩正则化；时间因子采用LSTM先验建模；变分推断通过展开优化算法实现；使用组独立成分分析（group ICA）进行热启动。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及轻量化方法。虽然提到"parameter-efficient architecture"，但这是指变分推断框架本身的参数效率，并非模型压缩/剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向神经影像分析，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成fMRI数据集（realistic synthetic fMRI dataset）；评价指标：潜在因子恢复质量（latent factor recovery）；实验设置：与经典和概率分解基准方法对比。

Experiments: 在合成fMRI数据上，ST-VTD相比代表性经典和概率分解基准方法显著提升了潜在因子恢复效果。摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 仅在合成数据上验证，缺乏真实fMRI数据实验；2. 与遥感/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴；3. 未讨论计算效率或实时性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然论文本身不涉及遥感目标检测，但以下思路可能有间接启发：1）张量分解中的低秩结构先验（LL1分解）可类比遥感多光谱/高光谱数据的低秩特性，或许可用于遥感数据压缩或特征提取；2）LSTM先验建模时序动态的思路，对遥感视频流或时序遥感数据（如SAR时序）的时空建模有潜在参考价值；3）展开优化算法的变分推断框架在参数效率上的设计思路，可类比轻量化网络设计中的展开迭代结构。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Modeling shared and subject-specific structure in multisubject spatiotemporal data remains challenging, particularly in neuroimaging, where both spatial and temporal patterns exhibit rich variability across subjects. Existing matrix and tensor decompositions provide interpretable factorizations, but rely on fixed multilinear structures or coupling schemes that may limit their flexibility in capturing complex variability. In this work, we introduce a spatiotemporal variational tensor decomposition (ST-VTD) framework that combines a tensor factorization generative model with structured priors to jointly represent spatial maps and temporal dynamics. Spatial factors are regularized to promote a low-rank structure inspired by the LL1 decomposition, while temporal factors are modeled using a learned Long short-term memory (LSTM)-based prior, enabling flexible and adaptive dynamics. Posterior inference is performed using an amortized variational formulation by unrolling iterations of an optimization algorithm, leading to an interpretable and parameter-efficient architecture. The proposed inference framework employs a warm-start strategy based on group independent component analysis, which we found to improve optimization performance. Experiments on a realistic synthetic functional MRI (fMRI) dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves latent factor recovery compared with representative classical and probabilistic decomposition benchmarks.

</details>


### [205] [General Value Functions for Remaining Useful Life and Failure-Mode Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22268)
*Hao Yan,Ali Sarabi,Qing Zou,Boyang Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出将剩余寿命预测和故障模式分类建模为向量广义价值函数（GVF）的时序强化学习问题，用多步时序差分估计器替代传统有监督固定窗口方法。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构，仅提到"same-backbone Monte Carlo control"作为对比基线，核心方法为向量GVF + TD(n,λ)时序差分估计器。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等部署相关信息。

Experiments: 在事件触发多模式仿真和NASA C-MAPSS标签稀缺拼接数据上，TD方法相比有监督同骨干网络蒙特卡洛控制方法在RUL和故障模式预测上均有提升，尤其在完整标签稀缺时优势更明显。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身未讨论模型效率、实时推理或边缘部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可能的间接启发是：时序差分（TD）学习的思想或许可用于遥感视频流中目标检测的时序一致性建模，但与本课题"轻量化"核心方向关联极弱，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and failure-mode classification are central tasks in predictive maintenance. Many data-driven pipelines use fixed-window supervised learning with complete terminal labels; such routes do not naturally encode the temporal recursion linking successive degradation-state predictions when observations are partial or unit identities are unavailable. We formulate prognostics as vector General Value Function (GVF) prediction on an absorbing degradation process, treating RUL and failure-mode probabilities as temporally consistent targets rather than independent window-level labels, and estimate them with a multi-step temporal-difference estimator, TD($n,λ$). Supporting theory identifies the Bellman fixed point of the vector GVFs, characterizes the linear projected-TD limit and its relation to complete-return Monte Carlo regression under realizability, and explains when bootstrapped TD targets are less variable than Monte Carlo returns. On an event-triggered multimode simulation and NASA C-MAPSS label-scarce stitch data, TD improves RUL and failure-mode prediction relative to a supervised same-backbone Monte Carlo control, especially under scarce complete labels. Practically, fragmented, identity-free degradation records can contribute local Bellman transitions instead of being discarded until complete run-to-failure labels are available.

</details>


### [206] [Hopformer: Homogeneity-Pursuit Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22299)
*Wan Zhang,Qinjie Lin,Chan Lee,Weijian Li,Han Liu,Kai Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Hopformer，一种面向高维协变量多时间序列预测的两阶段框架，通过稀疏模式聚合（SPA）提取公共趋势，再用LoRA微调Transformer建模残差依赖，在预测任务上取得SOTA。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于时间序列预测（time-series forecasting），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然使用了LoRA（一种轻量级微调方法），但应用场景和问题设定与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多时间序列预测（multi-time-series forecasting）；应用场景：通用时间序列预测；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 两阶段框架：第一阶段SPA（Sparsity Pattern Aggregation）提取公共低方差趋势；第二阶段LoRA微调Transformer建模残差依赖。

Lightweight method: 使用了LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）对Transformer进行参数高效微调，但这是针对微调效率而非推理轻量化，与剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。LoRA虽可减少微调参数量，但未讨论推理阶段的轻量化或边缘/星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 合成数据集和真实世界时间序列预测基准；评价指标：MASE（Mean Absolute Scaled Error）。

Experiments: 在多个预测基准上MASE平均提升6.56%，达到SOTA。提供了理论分析：SPA的oracle不等式证明近最优偏差-方差权衡，以及第二阶段在依赖时间序列数据下的泛化界。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关；LoRA方法虽可借鉴但应用场景不同；未讨论推理效率、模型大小等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：（1）LoRA作为参数高效微调方法，理论上可迁移到遥感检测模型的领域自适应或增量学习场景；（2）SPA的稀疏模式聚合思想或许可启发遥感特征中的公共模式提取，但需要大幅改造。整体借鉴价值较低。

Abstract: Forecasting multiple time-series with high-dimensional covariates presents a core challenge: unifying common temporal patterns while retaining meaningful series-specific information. We introduce Hopformer (Homogeneity-Pursuit Transformer), a two-stage framework that addresses this challenge. In the first stage, we perform a Sparsity Pattern Aggregation (SPA) scheme extracting a common low-variance trend that incorporates the covariates. This acts as a homogenization layer. In the second stage, a LoRA-fine-tuned Transformer models the remaining complex dependencies in the residual. Our method is theoretically grounded. We prove that SPA achieves a near-optimal bias-variance trade-off via an oracle inequality. We also provide generalization bounds for the second stage under dependent time series data. Hopformer sets a new state of the art, improving MASE by an average of 6.56% across synthetic and real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [207] [Learning Bidirectional Causal Interactions with Heteroscedastic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22313)
*Masahiro Tanaka*

Main category: stat.ML

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: SEM-DNN，基于神经网络的异方差同步方程估计器，利用条件协方差对角化实现结构参数识别，采用对角高斯拟似然函数，包含同步系统Jacobian项。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或实时推理技术。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：蒙特卡洛模拟数据（非线性、高维干扰函数、非高斯冲击）以及ready-to-eat cereal scanner data（零售扫描数据）；评价指标：结构效应恢复的可靠性（reliability in recovering structural effects）。

Experiments: 蒙特卡洛实验表明SEM-DNN在信息量增加时比参数方法、核方法和单独方程神经网络方法更可靠地恢复结构效应，但计算成本更高。应用案例为价格-销售反馈分析。

Limitations: 摘要明确提到"greater computational cost"（更高的计算成本）；此外，该方法与遥感、视觉、目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无可借鉴点。论文讨论的是计量经济学中的结构方程建模，不涉及任何视觉任务、遥感数据或模型压缩技术。

Abstract: Estimating contemporaneous bidirectional interactions from observational data is difficult because each outcome is endogenous to the other, while flexible regressions may capture only reduced-form dependence. This paper proposes SEM-DNN, a heteroscedastic neural simultaneous-equation estimator that learns reciprocal structural interactions without external instruments. Identification exploits conditional covariance diagonalization: when structural shocks have zero conditional means, are conditionally uncorrelated given predetermined covariates, and exhibit nonproportional conditional variances, only the true interaction coefficients diagonalize the conditional residual covariance across the feature space. The method jointly approximates nonlinear structural mean functions and feature-dependent variances using a diagonal Gaussian quasi-likelihood that incorporates the simultaneous-system Jacobian. We establish unique identification and positive-definite local curvature of the profiled population criterion and show that, under neural-profile compatibility conditions, the implemented neural criterion inherits this curvature despite nonunique network parameterizations. The coefficients admit a causal interpretation when the structural equations represent autonomous mechanisms that remain invariant under the relevant interventions. Monte Carlo experiments with nonlinear, high-dimensional nuisance functions and non-Gaussian shocks show that SEM-DNN recovers structural effects more reliably than parametric, kernel-based, and separate-equation neural alternatives as information increases, although at greater computational cost. An application to ready-to-eat cereal scanner data illustrates how the method can study contemporaneous price-sales feedback and assess identification strength, residual diagonalization, variance calibration, and optimization sensitivity.

</details>


### [208] [Learning Ergodic Dynamical Systems from a Finite Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22399)
*Oleksii Kachaiev,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究遍历随机动力系统单条有限轨迹的预测函数学习与Koopman算子估计，属于统计学习理论与遍历理论的交叉工作。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是随机动力系统的统计学习理论（Koopman算子、遍历马尔可夫链的浓度不等式），属于纯数学/理论机器学习方向，不涉及任何视觉任务或工程部署问题。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何视觉数据集或评价指标。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于随机动力系统与统计学习理论，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning from a single finite trajectory of an ergodic stochastic dynamical system. More precisely, we study discrete-time autonomous stochastic systems defining time-homogeneous Markov processes. We first focus on estimating the optimal one-step prediction function by nonlinear least squares, and derive high-probability guarantees measured with respect to the invariant measure of the process. These results make explicit how the non-independent and non-identically distributed nature of trajectory data modifies the classical statistical learning analysis. We then extend the framework to higher-order systems and finite-state spaces. Finally, we show that the same least squares and concentration arguments naturally extend to learning Koopman operators. Our approach combines tools from statistical learning theory and quantitative ergodic theory for Markov chains. It relies, in particular, on a concentration inequality for Hilbert-space-valued additive functionals of uniformly geometrically ergodic Markov chains.

</details>

*Ali Fakhar,K{é}vin Polisano,Ir{è}ne Gannaz,Sophie Achard*

TL;DR: 提出一种基于凸优化的理论相关矩阵生成方法，通过向椭球面投影并施加半正定约束，生成具有指定稀疏模式（对应图结构）的相关矩阵，并允许控制非对角元素分布的均值。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是相关矩阵生成与图结构约束的凸优化方法，应用领域为神经科学和金融，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明遥感数据集。使用了神经科学和金融两个真实数据集，并与GAN-based相关矩阵生成方法进行了比较。评价指标未明确说明。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接联系是：如果未来研究涉及遥感数据中的相关矩阵分析（如多光谱波段相关性建模），该方法的可控稀疏相关矩阵生成思路可能有一定参考价值，但与本课题核心方向（目标检测+轻量化）无关。

Abstract: This work addresses the generation of theoretical correlation matrices with prescribed sparsity patterns associated to graph structures. We propose a novel convex optimization framework in which an initial matrix is projected onto an elliptope under a positive semidefiniteness constraint. Several numerical schemes are implemented and compared. The problem falls within the broader class of matrix completion, where off-diagonal entries corresponding to absent edges are fixed to zero and diagonal entries are fixed to one. Beyond this structural constraint, the approach offers greater flexibility than existing methods by allowing control over the mean of the off-diagonal entry distribution, enabling the generation of correlation matrices that better reflect realistic data. This procedure is not designed to yield a uniform distribution over the feasible set; rather, it provides a principled and tunable way to construct correlation matrices suitable for benchmarking statistical methods for graphical model inference. Theoretical guarantees on the existence of solutions are established, both in the general setting and under the additional mean constraint. Simulation studies illustrate the properties of the generated matrices with respect to graph structure. The methodology is applied to two real-world datasets from neuroscience and finance, and a comparison with GAN-based correlation matrix generation is provided.

</details>


### [210] [CausalForge: A Formally Grounded, Self-Improving Agentic Framework for Automated Research in Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22511)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为自动化因果推断理论研究，不涉及遥感、目标检测等场景。

Model architecture: CausalForge框架包含两个核心组件：Causalean（因果推断的Lean形式化库，含7,035个机器验证声明）和CausalSmith（自改进智能体流水线，负责选题、提出结果、形式化陈述、构造证明）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。使用自主研究运行产生的artifacts进行评估。

Experiments: 摘要未说明详细实验结果。仅提及使用自主研究运行产生的artifacts评估系统。

Limitations: 论文承认机器验证的证明仅能确保形式化陈述从其假设中推导得出，不能保证该陈述忠实表达了预期的科学主张，因此增加了陈述审计环节。

Ideas for my research: 与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴点。该论文属于因果推断的形式化验证方向，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩加速。

Abstract: Automating theoretical research is constrained not only by the generation of candidate results, but also by their reliable evaluation. A common approach is to close the research loop with a large language model (LLM) reviewer. However, such reviewers remain empirically unreliable: they may accept fabricated papers and detect them at rates close to chance (Bad Scientist, 2025). We present CausalForge, a framework for automated theoretical research in causal inference grounded in the Lean proof assistant. CausalForge combines Causalean, a foundational Lean library for causal inference containing 7,035 machine-checked declarations developed with language-model assistance under human design and review, with CausalSmith, a self-improving agentic pipeline that selects research topics, proposes results, formalizes statements, constructs proofs, and presents the resulting artifacts for human inspection. Because a machine-checked proof establishes only that a formal statement follows from its assumptions, not that the statement faithfully captures the intended scientific claim, the pipeline augments kernel verification with a statement audit that compares each formal theorem against the informal claim it is intended to express. We evaluate the system using artifacts produced by completed autonomous research runs. The source code, formal library, and run records are available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/CausalForge.

</details>
