### [211] [Learning Diverse Humanoid Tasks via Synthetic Video Scenarios without Real World Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21648)
*Yun-Hao Tsai,Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出利用生成式AI将文本提示转换为多样化人体运动序列，作为机器人模仿学习的训练数据，以解决人形机器人复杂技能获取中真实数据采集成本高、多样性不足的问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于人形机器人的模仿学习与运动技能获取，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化等方向均无任何关联。属于机器人学与生成式AI交叉领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人形机器人模仿学习（Learning-from-Demonstrations）；应用场景：机器人运动技能获取；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：人体运动序列。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及使用Generative AI将文本提示转换为人体运动序列，但未给出具体生成模型（如扩散模型、VAE、GAN等）的细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理或边缘/星上部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅在4个仿真场景（simulation scenarios）中评估，未提及真实数据。

Experiments: 在4个仿真场景中评估，机器人能成功完成任务并对复杂运动变化表现出强适应性。未提供定量对比结果或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显缺失：1）未讨论仿真到真实的迁移（sim-to-real）问题；2）未给出生成运动序列的质量评估；3）未与现有模仿学习方法进行定量对比；4）与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，生成式AI用于数据增强的思路（如生成多样化遥感图像或目标实例）可能有一定启发，但论文本身聚焦人体运动而非遥感图像，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: The human-like morphology of humanoid robots grants them exceptional potential for agile and versatile motor capabilities, but it also introduces significant challenges in acquiring complex skills. Traditional Learning-from-Demonstrations methods are often constrained by the high cost of collecting real-world data, the difficulty of capturing motion-specific behaviors, and the limited diversity of demonstrations across individuals. Moreover, even for the same task, humans may execute the motion in multiple distinct ways. In this paper, we propose a new framework that leverages the power of Generative AI to convert textual prompts into realistic and diverse sequences of human body movements, enabling the robot to observe multiple variations of how a single task can be performed. These synthetic demonstrations are then used as a training resource, allowing the robot to learn a broad range of task-execution styles without requiring direct human intervention. We evaluate the proposed method across four simulation scenarios. Experimental results show that the robot not only completes the tasks successfully but also demonstrates strong adaptability to complex variations in motion.

</details>


### [212] [Progress Reward Modeling for Robotic Learning: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21655)
*Jianshu Zhang,Keliang Wu,Haoran Lu,Anbang Liu,Ce Zhang,Weijie Yin,Chengxuan Qian,Xiyuan Yang,Zhenyu Pan,Guo Ye,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一篇关于机器人学习中的进度奖励建模（progress reward modeling）的综述论文，提出统一的分析框架，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是机器人学习（robotic learning）中的进度奖励建模，属于机器人/强化学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是机器人学习中的进度奖励信号建模，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文是综述性质，讨论进度奖励建模的统一框架，未给出具体模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。完全不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文讨论机器人学习中的进度奖励建模，未提及遥感或目标检测相关数据集与指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。作为综述论文，未给出具体实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 论文本身是综述，总结了当前进度奖励建模方法的局限性（缺乏统一框架、不同方法难以比较等），但与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人/强化学习领域的进度奖励建模综述，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Robotic learning takes place in dynamic environments with large behavior spaces. A terminal success signal only tells the robot whether the task is completed. It does not explain whether the current behavior is making progress, remaining unchanged, or undoing earlier progress. For this reason, recent studies have increasingly explored progress rewards that provide feedback during task execution. However, the current literature lacks a shared framework. Existing methods use different observations, goal specifications, output signals, supervision sources, and evaluation protocols. This makes it difficult to compare them and understand what their results actually validate. In this survey, we provide a unified view of progress reward modeling for robotic learning. We organize the field in three connected steps. We first study the interface of a progress model. This defines the problem from the outside by asking what information the model receives and what form of progress signal it produces. We then move inside the model and study the methods used to construct this signal. This reveals the different assumptions and mechanisms behind progress estimation and reward generation. Finally, we examine the data and benchmarks that support these methods. This shows how progress supervision is obtained and what different evaluations actually measure. Together, these three perspectives connect what a progress model is, how it is built, and how its quality is validated. We further summarize the main limitations of current approaches and discuss future research directions.

</details>


### [213] [GRACE: Gradient-Free Robot Action Generation via Combined Diffusion-MPPI Posterior Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21661)
*Leesai Park,Jiho HOng,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GRACE方法，将扩散策略与MPPI控制结合，通过无梯度方式引导机器人动作生成，解决非可微约束下的部署问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦机器人动作生成与部署约束，属于机器人控制/操作领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人动作序列生成（robot action generation）；应用场景：机器人操作与部署约束满足；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：未说明。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于预训练扩散策略（pretrained diffusion policy），结合MPPI（Model Predictive Path Integral）控制，构建成本条件引导后验分布。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及在真实7-DoF机械臂上验证，但未给出计算资源需求。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真环境（未说明具体数据集名称）和真实7-DoF机械臂实验；评价指标：成功率（success rates）、碰撞避免能力。

Experiments: 仿真中GRACE优于基于扩散和基于采样的基线方法；真实机械臂实验中，无引导的扩散策略每次均碰撞障碍物，GRACE成功避开。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化技术。

Abstract: Diffusion policies generate multimodal robot action sequences from demonstrations, but steering them toward deployment-time constraints typically relies on differentiable guidance costs. This excludes many practical safety constraints, such as binary collision checks, joint limits, and black-box rollout costs that are nondifferentiable. We propose Gradient-free Robot Action generation via Combined diffusion-MPPI posterior mean Estimation (GRACE), which guides a pretrained diffusion policy with Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control using only forward cost evaluations. Building on the common score-ascent structure of diffusion and MPPI, GRACE constructs a cost-conditioned guidance posterior at each reverse step and estimates its mean with a single MPPI update centered at the diffusion reverse mean. For differentiable costs, GRACE recovers conventional gradient guidance under a first-order, matched-covariance approximation. GRACE attains higher success rates than diffusion-based and sampling-based baselines in simulation. On a real 7-DoF manipulator, GRACE avoids a deployment-time obstacle that the unguided prior collides with in every trial. Code and experiment videos are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/w/grace-70BB/.

</details>


### [214] [Ordered Action Tokens for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21670)
*Chaoqi Liu,Yue Zhao,Haonan Chen,Xiaoshen Han,Jiawei Gao,Ehsan Adeli,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出有序动作分词化方法(OAT)，将连续机器人动作映射为离散token序列，用于视觉-运动策略。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人动作分词化(visuomotor policies, robot action tokenization)，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人动作分词化与视觉-运动策略；应用场景：机器人操控(仿真benchmark和真实世界)；传感器类型：视觉(visuomotor)；目标类别：机器人动作chunks。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: OAT使用transformer with registers、finite scalar quantization、ordering-inducing training mechanisms。将动作chunks离散化为有序token序列，早期token包含粗粒度控制信息，后期token细化残差细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文涉及token压缩(high compression)和推理时灵活性(anytime tradeoff)，但这是针对动作序列长度而非模型轻量化。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：5个仿真benchmark和真实世界设置，共60+任务。评价指标：policy performance (具体指标摘要未说明)。

Experiments: 在三种policy backbones上验证，涵盖60+任务，OAT在提供更强推理灵活性的同时保持良好策略性能。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能相关的点是"anytime tradeoff between inference cost and action fidelity"的思想——在遥感目标检测中可类比为"推理时根据资源约束动态调整检测精度与速度的权衡"，但该论文方法本身(动作分词化)无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务。

Abstract: Action tokenization maps continuous robot action chunks to discrete tokens and has become an important interface for modern visuomotor policies. Existing approaches either rely on analytical discretization methods that produce prohibitively long token sequences or learned latent tokenizers that lack structure, limiting their compatibility with downstream policies. In this work, we identify three desiderata for action tokenization - high compression, total decodability, and an ordered token space - and introduce Ordered Action Tokenization (OAT), a learned action tokenizer that satisfies all three. OAT discretizes action chunks into an ordered sequence of tokens using a transformer with registers, finite scalar quantization, and ordering-inducing training mechanisms. By training each token prefix to decode into a valid action chunk, OAT places coarse control information in early tokens and uses later tokens to refine residual detail, yielding an anytime tradeoff between inference cost and action fidelity. We validate OAT in two prevailing uses of action tokens: autoregressive policies that generate tokens for control, and token co-training policies that use token losses to shape the vision-language model context consumed by a flow-based action expert. Across three policy backbones and more than 60 tasks spanning five simulation benchmarks and real-world settings, OAT consistently delivers strong policy performance while offering significantly greater flexibility at inference time.

</details>


### [215] [Addressing the Orchestration Gap in Generalist Robots via Physical Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21725)
*Liane Galanti,Dhruv Shah,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Pigey的物理智能体编排器，将通用机器人任务分解为高层规划与低层控制，无需额外训练即可提升现有视觉-语言-动作策略的推理能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦通用机器人操控（robotic manipulation），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用机器人操控任务（robotic manipulation）；应用场景：仿真环境（LIBERO-PRO）和真实机器人；传感器类型：视觉（vision）；目标类别：机器人操控对象。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: Pigey（Physical Agency orchestrator）：一个高层智能体编排器，包含高层规划、子目标分解、低层运动指令控制、观测跟踪与验证、失败恢复等模块；底层可调用现有的VLA（vision-language-action）策略或参数化技能。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真基准：LIBERO-PRO；真实机器人操控任务。评价指标：任务成功率（success rate）。

Experiments: 在LIBERO-PRO上，Pigey将SOTA从12.8%提升至53.3%（提升超4倍），无需任务特定微调；在真实机器人上，将冻结策略从接近零提升至超过90%的推理受限任务成功率。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术；论文聚焦机器人操控的推理与规划，非视觉感知任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一的间接启发是"编排器/调度器"思想——在星上资源受限场景中，或许可以用类似的高层调度策略来动态选择不同轻量级检测模型，但论文本身未涉及任何遥感或轻量化技术。

Abstract: General-purpose robots need to reason about their actions, combining perception, world knowledge, planning, success detection, recovery, and low-level control. Today's state-of-the-art models attempt to combine all these capabilities into the learned policy via large-scale pre-training. Instead, we show that these capabilities can be decomposed into a general language-conditioned policy/control agent and a high-level agent manager/orchestrator. Rather than training policies to reason via pre-training, we build a closed-loop physical agent orchestrator that can do high-level planning, decompose the goal into achievable subgoals, command low-level motor commands, track and verify the outcome from low-level observations, and recover from failures. Our Physical Agency orchestrator (Pigey) can control existing vision-language-action (VLA) policies as well as parametrized skills to solve complex reasoning tasks in the real world, without any additional data collection or post-training. We evaluate Pigey extensively across simulation benchmarks and challenging real-world robotic manipulation tasks, and demonstrate significant performance improvements over existing generalist policies. On LIBERO-PRO, Pigey advances the state-of-the-art by over 4x (12.8% -> 53.3%) with no task-specific fine-tuning. On a real robot, Pigey lifts the frozen policy from near-zero to over 90% on reasoning-limited tasks. We call the difference between what frozen motor skills achieve alone and inside the agentic loop the orchestration gap.

</details>


### [216] [StARS: Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via a Recommender System-Driven Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21802)
*Erencem Ozbey,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Jin Huang,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于协同过滤的模型无关框架StARS，用于人机交互中个性化社交适宜性动作生成。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是人机交互（HRI）中的社交适宜性动作生成问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。属于泛机器人/人机交互方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：社交适宜性动作生成（socially appropriate action generation）；场景：人机交互（HRI）；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: StARS框架：协同过滤（collaborative filtering）+ 可学习场景表征（learnable scene representations），模型无关（model-agnostic），可集成多种场景编码器和骨干网络。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MannersDB+、SocNav1；评价指标：性能（performance）和标注者一致性（agreement with annotators）。

Experiments: 在MannersDB+和SocNav1数据集上，StARS在不同骨干网络下均能提升性能和标注者一致性，并在稀疏偏好反馈下具有鲁棒性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的遥感或轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文研究的是人机交互中的个性化偏好建模，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Social appropriateness in human-robot interaction (HRI) is not universal: different people can judge the same robot action differently in the same situation. To capture this inter-subject variability, we reformulate socially appropriate action generation as a preference modelling problem inspired by recommender systems, treating annotators as users, contexts/scenes as items, and appropriateness scores over a set of candidate robot actions as targets. We propose StARS, a novel model-agnostic framework that integrates collaborative filtering with learnable scene representations to generate user-specific appropriateness scores over candidate robot actions. StARS is model-agnostic: it can be integrated with various scene encoders and backbones, enabling personalisation without redesigning the underlying model. We evaluate StARS on two socially aware robotics datasets, MannersDB+ and SocNav1, and analyse robustness under sparse preference feedback. Across datasets and backbones, StARS consistently improves performance and agreement with annotators, supporting personalised action selection aligned with user norms. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/StARS.git.

</details>


### [217] [Action-Conditioned World Model for Goal Plane Probe Guidance in Robotic Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21918)
*Siqi Fan,Mingcong Chen,Ran Liu,Zixuan Yang,Xiaoyu Fu,Xiaoqing Gao,Yunhui Liu,Hongbin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动作条件扩散世界模型的机器人超声自主扫描框架，用于颈动脉/甲状腺超声探头导航，通过两阶段模型学习实现目标导向的探头运动规划。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是机器人超声（robotic ultrasound）中的探头导航问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人超声探头目标导向导航（goal plane probe guidance）；场景：颈部超声扫描（颈动脉/甲状腺）；传感器：超声探头；目标类别：解剖结构（颈动脉、甲状腺）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 两阶段模型：1）潜在条件扩散世界模型（latent conditional diffusion world model），用于预测未来超声观测；2）目标条件时序Transformer（goal-conditioned temporal transformer），用于预测有序探头运动。摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs等。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自采集数据集（self-collected dataset）；评价指标：成功率（success rate），颈动脉引导70.0%，甲状腺引导65.0%。

Experiments: 在自采集数据集上训练世界模型，在真实世界闭环实验中验证，颈动脉引导成功率70.0%，甲状腺引导成功率65.0%。摘要未提供与基线方法的对比或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2）仅在自采集数据集上验证，泛化性未知；3）成功率仍有较大提升空间（65%-70%）；4）未讨论模型计算开销和实时性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。扩散世界模型用于预测未来观测这一思路在遥感领域可能有一定启发（如预测卫星未来成像结果辅助规划），但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向关联度极低。

Abstract: We present an action-conditioned world model framework for goal plane probe guidance in robotic ultrasound, with a focus on neck ultrasound scanning. Autonomous ultrasound tasks often require large numbers of probe-motion trajectories for training, but collecting high-quality demonstrations is labor-intensive and explicit simulators are difficult to build because ultrasound appearance depends on contact, tissue deformation, and view-dependent acoustic artifacts. We address this problem with a two-stage model-based learning pipeline. First, a latent conditional diffusion world model predicts future ultrasound observations from recent context frames, probe motions and temporal offset. Second, a goal-conditioned temporal transformer predicts ordered probe motions and is fine-tuned using rewards from the frozen world model. Experiments on the self-collected dataset show that the world model preserves action-dependent anatomical structure on target-directed scans. In real-world closed loop experiments, the framework achieves success rates of 70.0\% for carotid guidance and 65.0\% for thyroid guidance. These results demonstrate the potential of learned ultrasound dynamics for training goal-directed robotic probe navigation.

</details>


### [218] [Adaptive Undulatory Locomotion of Snake-like Robots in Dynamic Viscous Environments via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21960)
*Tsuyoshi Kimoto,Akio Yamano,Kohei Honda,Takashi Iwasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用深度强化学习(DRL)和特权信息蒸馏，使蛇形机器人在动态粘性环境中实现自适应运动，超越了传统预定义控制方法的性能极限。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是蛇形机器人在粘性流体环境中的自适应运动控制，属于机器人学与强化学习的交叉领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级极低。除非对特权信息蒸馏在受限传感器场景下的应用有特殊兴趣，否则不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：蛇形机器人自适应运动控制；场景：动态粘性流体环境；传感器：本体感知传感器(proprioceptive sensor)；目标类别：无目标检测任务。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、航空图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关要素。

Model architecture: 采用非对称actor-critic框架(asymmetric actor-critic framework)，包含teacher policy（使用特权信息训练）和student policy（仅依赖本体感知信息）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation)方法——将teacher policy的知识蒸馏到student policy中。但此处的蒸馏目的是解决部分可观测问题(POMDP)，而非模型轻量化或压缩。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到了student policy仅依赖本体感知信息，具有一定的部署便利性，但完全不涉及星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真环境：动态粘度范围10⁻⁷至10⁻² m²/s；评价指标：推进速度(propulsion velocity)、运输效率(transport efficiency)。无遥感领域标准数据集。

Experiments: 仿真实验表明DRL智能体自主习得非正弦自适应步态，在推进速度和运输效率上超越传统正弦控制和运动学控制方法。摘要未说明与SOTA方法的详细对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 仅在仿真环境中验证，缺乏实物实验；2. 仅针对蛇形机器人和粘性流体场景，泛化性未知；3. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；4. 摘要未说明蒸馏后的student policy在参数量、推理速度等方面的量化收益。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文主题与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但以下思路有微弱借鉴价值：1. 特权信息蒸馏(asymmetric actor-critic + privileged information distillation)的思想——在遥感在轨场景中，可以在地面训练时利用高精度标注或高分辨率先验信息(privileged info)训练teacher模型，再蒸馏到仅依赖星上低分辨率/低质量输入的student模型；2. 将部分可观测问题(POMDP)建模思路迁移到星上传感器受限场景。但整体借鉴价值有限，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates how deep reinforcement learning (DRL) enables adaptive locomotion of snake-like robots in dynamically changing viscous environments, overcoming the inherent performance limitations of classical predefined control methods. The lack of direct onboard sensors for fluid properties necessitates formulating this task as a partially observable Markov decision process. By employing an asymmetric actor-critic framework, a teacher policy trained using privileged information available only in the physics simulator distills its knowledge into a student policy that relies solely on proprioceptive sensor information. Simulation results across a wide range of dynamic viscosity changes ($10^{-7}$ to $10^{-2} m^2/s$) reveal that the DRL agent autonomously acquires non-sinusoidal adaptive gaits. These gaits improve propulsion velocity and transport efficiency, breaking the inherent limits of conventional sinusoidal and kinematic control. The findings establish that implicit environment inference via privileged information distillation is an effective approach to bypass the constraints of classical models under unpredictable fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [219] [ACME: A Multi-Cultural, Multi-Embodiment Social-Navigation Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21964)
*Shashank Rao Marpally,Allan Wang,Atharva Ghotavadekar,Renato Alexandre Ribeiro,Nhat Le,Pilar Bachiller-Burgos,Pranav Goyal,Subham Agrawal,Yasuhiro Nitta,Howard Ziyu Han,Daeun Song,Masaki Kuribayashi,Kohei Uehara,Xiyue Wang,Yangzhe Kong,Duc M. Nguyen,Amirreza Payandeh,Gerardo Pérez-González,Alejandro Torrejón-Harto,Jeeho Ahn,Tisha Jain,Andrew Stratton,Elvin Yang,Jorge de Heuvel,Nico Ostermann-Myrau,Sai Anudeep Sajja,Mithilya Raj,Daisuke Sato,Gaston Rouquette,Nikolas Martelaro,Maki Sugimoto,Hironobu Takagi,Chieko Asakawa,Maren Bennewitz,Aaron Steinfeld,Xuesu Xiao,Christoforos Mavrogiannis,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个跨文化、多形态的社交导航数据集ACME，用于研究机器人与人类在共享空间中的移动行为，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是社交机器人导航（social robot navigation），属于机器人学与人类行为理解领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心概念，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 社交机器人导航（social robot navigation）、行人轨迹预测（pedestrian trajectory prediction）、人机交互（human-robot interaction）。传感器类型：机器人车载传感器（onboard robot data）和俯视行人追踪数据（overhead pedestrian tracking）。不涉及遥感传感器或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文主要贡献是数据集，未提出具体模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是社交导航场景下的机器人部署，而非星上/边缘部署，未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: ACME数据集：跨文化（5个国家8个站点）、多形态（7种机器人）、多模态（3D/2D场景特征、里程计、交互信息、行人轨迹标注）。包含29.35小时车载数据和43.5小时俯视行人追踪数据。定性+定量分析。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及对数据集的定性和定量分析，表明ACME比先前数据集包含更具挑战性的场景和更广泛的行人行为分布。未涉及目标检测或轻量化实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何技术参考。论文本身作为社交导航数据集，可能存在的局限包括：未涉及遥感场景、未提供目标检测标注、未讨论模型效率问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于社交机器人导航领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线和应用场景均不匹配。

Abstract: Understanding how robots and humans move in shared spaces is essential for designing effective social robot navigation policies and predicting human behavior. However, existing datasets often lack the diversity needed to capture differences in culture, geography, and human-robot interaction-factors that strongly shape appropriate social behavior. To address this gap, we introduce ACME: A Cross-cultural, Multi-Embodiment dataset for social navigation. A large-scale data collection effort across 8 sites in 5 countries, using 7 robot embodiments, ACME is a large and diverse multi-modal dataset aimed at advancing social navigation research, providing 29.35 hours of onboard robot data and 43.5 hours of overhead pedestrian tracking data. Unlike prior datasets, it focuses on capturing goal-driven social navigation behavior in complex social scenarios with explicit robot-crowd interaction through robot speech. To facilitate learning navigation policies and predicting pedestrian trajectories, ACME provides 3D and 2D scene features, odometry, interaction information, and human-annotated pedestrian trajectory labels. We make ACME easy to use by providing both human-readable data for each sensor modality as well as raw binary data. Our qualitative and quantitative analyses show that our dataset captures more challenging scenarios and a broader distribution of pedestrian behavior than previous datasets.

</details>


### [220] [Mag4D-SLAM Dataset: A Repeated-Traversal Multi-Modal 4D Geomagnetic Dataset for Localization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21986)
*Bibhutibhusan Nayak,Hyoseok Ju,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了首个大规模室外地磁SLAM数据集Mag4D-SLAM，包含多模态传感器同步数据，用于地磁导航与SLAM研究。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于地磁SLAM（同步定位与建图）数据集构建，完全不涉及遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：地磁SLAM数据集构建；场景：室外结构化校园环境；传感器：LiDAR、相机、IMU、三轴磁力计、GNSS；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文核心贡献是数据集而非模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Mag4D-SLAM，14个序列，总长18km+，包含LiDAR、相机、IMU、磁力计、GNSS数据及SE(3)真值；评价指标：磁场重复性、全局航向估计精度、位置判别能力。

Experiments: 通过重复遍历实验分析了磁场在不同记录时段（白天/夜晚）的重复性、无漂移全局航向估计能力、跨时段地点识别的磁特征判别性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型、方法或部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人SLAM/导航领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接关联。

Abstract: Geomagnetic sensing offers an infrastructure-free, absolute orientation reference that is robust to GNSS denial and visual degradation, yet no large-scale outdoor robotics dataset supports its systematic study in SLAM. Existing magnetic datasets are confined to small-scale indoor environments and lack the synchronized multi-modal sensing, repeated-traversal structure, and high-precision 6-DoF ground truth required for geomagnetic SLAM research. We present Mag4D-SLAM, the first large-scale outdoor geomagnetic SLAM dataset. It comprises 14 sequences totaling over 18 km of synchronized LiDAR, camera, IMU, tri-axis magnetometer, and GNSS measurements with SE(3) ground-truth poses, collected along structured campus trajectories under paired day/night conditions in both forward and reverse directions. Through repeated-traversal experiments, we analyze three core properties: magnetic field repeatability across different recording sessions (daytime and nighttime), drift-free global heading estimation, and location-discriminative magnetic signatures for cross-session place recognition. Mag4D-SLAM is designed to support research on yaw drift mitigation, magnetic loop closure, and long-term localization and to open new research questions on how geomagnetic sensing can complement visual and LiDAR modalities or provide a fallback cue under illumination changes, structural repetition, and GNSS-denied long-term operation.

</details>


### [221] [Embodying Multi-Hand Manipulation Policies by Searching the Assignment and Null Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22020)
*Yorai Shaoul,Jiaoyang Li,Maxim Likhachev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于冲突搜索的多臂机器人运动规划框架，将抽象的多手策略输出映射到物理多臂系统上，解决轨迹分配与避碰问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于多臂机器人运动规划与逆运动学求解，属于机器人操作领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 多臂机器人运动规划、轨迹分配、避碰；场景为机器人操作；传感器类型和目标类别均不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Conflict-Based Search的搜索框架，统一处理离散轨迹分配与连续Jacobian零空间运动。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。该论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术点。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文研究的是机器人运动规划问题，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Learned manipulation policies increasingly predict motions for abstract "hands" and are attractive in practice because they rely on easily collected demonstrations and transfer across robot platforms. Executing these trajectories on multi-arm robots, however, is not trivial. Multi-hand policy outputs must be assigned to physical arms, each arm must realize a configuration-space motion that tracks its prescribed end-effector trajectory, and all arms must respect kinematic limits and avoid collisions. In the absence of algorithms that directly address this problem, practitioners typically extend single-arm inverse-kinematics (IK) pipelines in an ad hoc way, with no guarantees of feasibility or safety. In this work, we close this execution gap with a search-based framework that is theoretically complete for grounding policy-generated multi-hand trajectories onto physical multi-arm systems. Building on Conflict-Based Search, our method explicitly searches over both the discrete assignment of trajectories to arms and the continuous Jacobian null spaces of redundant manipulators, using redundancy to avoid inter-arm collisions while tracking the prescribed motions. This unified treatment of assignment and null-space motion yields a practically efficient planner that safely realizes coordinated manipulation-policy outputs on multi-arm robots. See omcbsa.github.io for more.

</details>


### [222] [Impedance Control of Ship-Borne Manipulators via Optimization-based Task-Space Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22030)
*Lingxiao Meng,Bi-Ke Zhu,Xuheng Gao,Zhe Zhang,Jiankun Yang,Jiankun Wang,Haibo Lu,Max Q. -H. Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于TSID和ESKF的船载机械臂力矩级优化控制框架，用于补偿海浪引起的基座运动扰动，提升轨迹跟踪精度和柔顺操作能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦船载机械臂的运动控制和阻抗控制，属于机器人/海洋工程领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：船载机械臂轨迹跟踪与柔顺控制（peg-in-hole插入）；应用场景：海洋环境船载操作；传感器类型：IMU+末端位姿反馈；目标类别：无。摘要未说明遥感/卫星/目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 控制框架：基于TSID（task-space inverse dynamics）的QP优化控制器 + ESKF（error-state Kalman filter）基座状态估计器。摘要未说明神经网络模型结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验方式：仿真+实物实验（7-DOF机械臂安装在6-DOF Stewart平台上）；评价指标：末端位置跟踪误差（降低25.7%）、插入成功率、平均接触力（降低45%）。摘要未说明遥感数据集。

Experiments: 与最佳基线相比，末端位置跟踪误差降低25.7%；在1mm间隙的peg-in-hole插入任务中，成功率提升且平均接触力降低45%。摘要未说明消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Ship-borne manipulators operating in maritime environments are subject to stochastic wave-induced base motions that introduce kinematic disturbances and dynamic coupling, degrading trajectory tracking accuracy and complicating safe, contact-rich manipulation. This paper proposes a torque-level optimization-based control framework that integrates high-precision trajectory tracking with task-space impedance for ship-borne manipulators. The controller is formulated using task-space inverse dynamics (TSID) and solved via quadratic programming to explicitly compensate for the dynamic coupling introduced by base motion. To enable accurate feedforward compensation, an error-state Kalman filter (ESKF) is developed to estimate the base state by fusing inertial measurements with end-effector pose feedback. The framework is validated in simulation and real-world experiments using a 7-DOF manipulator mounted on a 6-DOF Stewart platform. The proposed method reduces real-world end-effector position tracking error by over 25.7% compared with the best baseline. Furthermore, the controller enables dynamic peg-in-hole insertion with 1~mm clearance under base motion, increasing the success rate while reducing average contact forces by 45%, demonstrating precise and compliant manipulation in contact-rich environments.

</details>


### [223] [One Hand Watches The Other: Dynamic Multi-Agent Cooperation for Sample-Efficient Bimanual Manipulation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22119)
*Jan Ole von Hartz,Abhinav Valada,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DynaMAC框架，解决多流机器人操作策略在动态环境中的因果假设问题，实现零样本泛化，但完全与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人操作（robot manipulation）、双臂协调（bimanual coordination）和动态环境中的策略学习，属于机器人学与强化学习/模仿学习范畴。完全不涉及遥感（remote sensing）、卫星（satellite）、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；也不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；虽然标题提到"lightweight"，但这里的轻量是指策略框架的轻量，与模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）或边缘/星上部署无关。因此与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人动态操作与双臂协调策略学习。应用场景：机器人操作、人机协作。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：机器人臂、动态物体。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: DynaMAC框架，policy-agnostic（策略无关），将对手臂视为动态任务参数（dynamic task parameter），统一了动态操作和双臂协调的公式化表达。不涉及遥感目标检测模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到"lightweight, policy-agnostic framework"，但这里的轻量是指框架设计上的简洁高效，并非模型压缩领域的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。不涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。提到"computational speed"和"20 times fewer samples"（样本效率），但这是训练样本效率而非推理效率。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 提出DynaBench基准测试，用于动态环境中的机器人操作。评价指标：相比领先的概率和生成式基线方法提升超过35个百分点。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 在动态环境和双臂操作任务上，DynaMAC优于领先的概率和生成式基线方法超过35个百分点，所需样本减少20倍。能从静态演示零样本泛化到动态环境。与遥感目标检测实验无关。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。摘要本身未说明该方法在遥感场景下的局限性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接借鉴意义。唯一可能间接参考的是其"将动态因素作为参数统一建模"的思想，但应用场景差异巨大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Multi-stream robot manipulation policies achieve unparalleled sample efficiency and generalization by modeling actions relative to environmental reference frames. However, existing approaches typically assume these frames to be strictly exogenous. This causal assumption collapses in dynamic settings, such as when a single robot arm manipulates a moving object or when two arms coordinate, where each arm effectively becomes part of the dynamic environment of the other. We propose DynaMAC, a lightweight, policy-agnostic framework that resolves this causal limitation while preserving the sample efficiency, computational speed, and flexibility of multi-stream policies, DynaMAC treats the opposite arm as a dynamic task parameter, thereby providing a unified formulation for dynamic manipulation and bimanual coordination without requiring an explicit leader-follower relationship. To rigorously evaluate these capabilities, we introduce DynaBench, a novel benchmark for robot manipulation in dynamic environments. Across both dynamic environments and bimanual manipulation tasks, DynaMAC outperforms leading probabilistic and generative baselines by over 35 percentage points while requiring 20 times fewer samples. Crucially, DynaMAC generalizes zero-shot from static demonstrations to dynamic environments, substantially simplifying data collection and establishing an elegant bridge toward human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [224] [DB-VIO: Dual-Branch Visual Inertial Odometry with Enhanced Visual-Inertial Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22123)
*Ziyu Wan,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DB-VIO双分支视觉惯性里程计框架，通过深度线索增强单目视觉、显式姿态先验增强惯性表征、解耦旋转和平移估计，在自动驾驶和空中机器人数据集上取得SOTA性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是视觉惯性里程计(VIO)用于6-DoF运动估计，属于机器人定位与导航领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然实验涉及aerial robot（空中机器人），但任务本质是运动估计而非遥感图像理解。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文研究VIO运动估计，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视觉惯性里程计(VIO)，6-DoF运动估计；场景：自动驾驶、空中机器人；传感器：单目相机+IMU；目标：旋转和平移的位姿估计。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: DB-VIO双分支框架：1) 深度线索增强单目视觉感知；2) 显式集成姿态先验(integrated-attitude prior)增强旋转感知的惯性表征；3) 解耦为旋转分支和翻译分支进行运动特异性时序建模。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：KITTI（自动驾驶）、EuRoC（空中机器人）；评价指标：相对位姿误差（旋转和平移），具体数值：KITTI上提升20%，EuRoC上提升33%，旋转指标提升65.7%。

Experiments: 在KITTI和EuRoC上达到SOTA，相比基线方法在KITTI提升20%，EuRoC提升33%；在EuRoC更敏捷的运动模式下旋转指标提升65.7%。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2) 未涉及任何轻量化或模型压缩技术；3) 未讨论在轨/边缘部署可行性；4) 未提供计算开销信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1) 双分支解耦设计思路（旋转/平移解耦）可类比遥感旋转框检测中的角度回归与位置回归解耦；2) 深度线索增强视觉感知的思路可用于遥感小目标检测中的特征增强。但整体与课题方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Visual inertial odometry (VIO) is essential for accurate 6-DoF motion estimation in mobile robotic systems. Recent learning-based VIO methods have shown promising progress, but they often rely on unified visual--inertial representations and a single temporal model for full-pose estimation, limiting their ability to capture the heterogeneous dynamics of rotation and translation. Moreover, monocular visual features often lack explicit geometric structure, while raw inertial encoding leaves the underlying rotational kinematics implicit, weakening the rotation-related cues in IMU features. To address these issues, we propose DB-VIO, a dual-branch visual inertial odometry framework with enhanced visual--inertial representation. DB-VIO incorporates depth cues to improve monocular visual perception, injects an explicit integrated-attitude prior to strengthen rotation-aware inertial representation, and decouples pose estimation into dedicated rotational and translational branches for motion-specific temporal modeling. Experiments on autonomous driving and aerial robot benchmarks show that DB-VIO achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving the corresponding baselines by 20\% on KITTI and 33\% on EuRoC. Notably, under the more agile motion patterns of EuRoC, DB-VIO improves the rotational metric by 65.7\% over prior methods. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of DB-VIO across different platforms and motion scenarios.

</details>


### [225] [Flight-Ready LiDAR-Inertial Odometry for Embedded Drone Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22145)
*Alvaro J. Gaona,David Perez-Saura,Francisco J. Anguita,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对基于IESKF的LIO系统（如FAST-LIO2）在无人机实时闭环控制中的五个架构缺陷进行改进，将里程计输出从10 Hz提升至200 Hz，并增加速度发布、平滑处理、双执行器隔离和同步保护。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于LiDAR-inertial odometry（LIO）在无人机实时控制中的架构优化，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。虽然涉及"onboard UAV"部署，但核心是状态估计与传感器融合，而非遥感图像理解或目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。除非对无人机机载系统的实时架构设计有额外兴趣，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LiDAR-惯性里程计（LIO）实时闭环控制；应用场景：无人机（UAV）自主飞行；传感器：LiDAR（Livox Mid-360）+ IMU（Pixhawk 4 Mini）；不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、场景理解。

Model architecture: 基于IESKF（迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波器）+ ikd-Tree的紧耦合LIO架构（FAST-LIO2衍生）。改进点包括：IMU速率前向传播、直接机体坐标系速度发布、SLERP平滑、双执行器隔离、显式同步保护。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等方法。

Onboard deployability: 涉及机载部署（onboard UAV），但关注的是LIO系统的实时性和频率（10 Hz→200 Hz），而非深度学习模型的推理延迟、参数量、FLOPs、显存或能耗。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验使用Livox Mid-360 / Pixhawk 4 Mini自主无人机平台，动捕系统（motion-capture）作为真值。评价指标：里程计输出频率（Hz）、状态估计连续性、LiDAR丢失时的鲁棒性。未使用遥感目标检测标准数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）。

Experiments: 在真实无人机平台上验证了改进后的系统：里程计输出从~10 Hz稳定提升至200 Hz，每个IMU采样点提供完整Twist状态，在LiDAR短暂丢失期间保持连续性。底层估计器（IESKF + ikd-Tree）保持不变，改进可直接应用于FAST-LIO2衍生实现。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何图像/遥感图像理解任务；2. 不涉及深度学习模型压缩或加速；3. 不涉及星上部署场景；4. 未讨论模型参数量、计算量等轻量化核心指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1. 双执行器隔离（dual-executor isolation）思想可类比于在轨推理中分离"感知"与"控制"任务，避免资源竞争；2. IMU速率前向传播的思路可类比于在轨目标检测中利用时序信息进行帧间预测补偿；3. 对"实时性瓶颈"的系统级分析思路（识别架构缺陷而非仅优化模型本身）值得借鉴，在星上部署时也需考虑I/O、同步、调度等系统级问题。

Abstract: Open-source LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) systems have achieved remarkable benchmark accuracy, yet current state-of-the-art implementations are primarily optimized for evaluation performance rather than the requirements of real-time closed-loop aerial control. When deployed onboard UAVs, this can introduce limitations that degrade flight performance. In this work, we identify five architectural deficiencies in a representative tightly coupled IESKF-based LIO implementation: odometry publishing tied to the LiDAR rate (10 Hz instead of the IMU's 200 Hz), missing velocity outputs, execution bottlenecks that block IMU processing, mutex contention, and synchronization race conditions. We introduce corresponding modifications including IMU-rate forward propagation, direct body-frame velocity publishing, SLERP-based smoothing, dual-executor isolation, and explicit synchronization protection. The resulting system increases odometry output from ~10 Hz to a stable 200 Hz, provides a complete Twist state at every IMU sample, and preserves continuity during transient LiDAR loss. Experiments on a Livox Mid-360 / Pixhawk 4 Mini autonomous UAV with motion-capture ground truth validate the approach. Since the underlying estimator (IESKF + ikd-Tree) remains unchanged, the proposed improvements can be directly applied to FAST-LIO2-derived implementations.

</details>


### [226] [Learning Spatiotemporal Decision Priors for Efficient Path Planning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22166)
*Yi Liu,Hongda Zhang,Leyao Zou,Chunlei Meng,Ziqing Zhou,Yuning Chen,Zhuo Zou,Lida Xu,Zhongxue Gan,Chun Ouyang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ImiPath框架，利用模仿学习从历史轨迹中提取时空决策先验，引导部分可观测环境下的路径规划，提升搜索效率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人/智能体在部分可观测环境下的路径规划问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化任何核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：路径规划（path planning）与导航决策；场景：部分可观测环境（partial observability）；传感器：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: STAPNet（SpatioTemporal-Attention Policy Network），编码局部空间信息和历史轨迹时序信息，输出决策先验（decision priors）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅在磁控微机器人平台上做了物理实验验证适应性，但与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：路径质量（path quality）、搜索效率（search efficiency）、冗余节点扩展减少（reducing redundant node expansions）。

Experiments: 大量实验表明ImiPath在部分可观测条件下实现了有竞争力的路径质量并提升了搜索效率；在磁控微机器人平台上进行了额外的物理实验验证。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴；摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等关键部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：其"从历史轨迹中提取先验引导搜索"的思路，在遥感在轨场景中可类比为"利用历史检测结果或时序上下文引导当前帧的检测注意力"，但这属于非常间接的联想，且论文本身不涉及检测任务。

Abstract: Path planning under partial observability remains challenging because an agent must make long-horizon navigation decisions from only locally bounded observations. Nevertheless, historical trajectories contain reusable experience-guided directional preferences. Classical planners, however, typically solve each instance from scratch and lack an explicit mechanism to exploit such transferable decision knowledge, often leading to redundant node expansions and locally myopic search behaviors. Motivated by this limitation, this paper proposes ImiPath, a prior-guided learning framework that distills reusable spatiotemporal decision priors from demonstration trajectories and uses them as experience-informed directional guidance to bias planners toward reliable and promising search directions under partial observability. Specifically, ImiPath first constructs a local spatiotemporal observation representation, which encodes the spatial information of the local environment and the temporal information of historical trajectories. The SpatioTemporal-Attention Policy Network (STAPNet) then transforms this representation into dicision priors. These priors are further incorporated into heterogeneous planners as directional guidance, biasing the search toward locally promising regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ImiPath achieves competitive path quality and improves search efficiency by reducing redundant node expansions under local observability. Additional physical experiments on a magnetic microrobot platform further validate the adaptability and practical deployment potential of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [227] [Safe Learning Predictive Control for Ego-World Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22225)
*Davide Valenti,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SOWL-MPC，一种基于稀疏变分高斯过程在线学习的预测控制策略，用于未知策略的世界机器人与自我机器人共存的导航场景。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于机器人安全导航与控制领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人安全自主导航与预测控制；场景：共享环境中的ego-world机器人交互；传感器类型：噪声状态测量（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: SOWL-MPC架构：Sparse Variational Gaussian Processes (SVGPs)在线学习 + Online Variational Conditioning (OVC) + 近似矩传播 + uncertainty-aware Model Predictive Control (MPC)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到在ROS 2中进行了蒙特卡洛虚拟实验并在真实机器人硬件上验证，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验方式：蒙特卡洛虚拟实验（ROS 2）+ 真实室内场地机器人实验；评价指标：安全性与实时可行性（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 通过蒙特卡洛仿真和真实硬件实验验证了SOWL-MPC的实时可行性和安全性，但摘要未给出定量对比结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation in shared environments requires the ability to anticipate and react to the latent behaviors of surrounding robots. In this paper, we propose SOWL-MPC, a safe learning-based predictive control strategy for a novel scenario, which we name ego-world robotic framework. In this setting, the control policy of the world robot is unknown and the ego exploits data to learn it and perform safe maneuvers. The proposed architecture combines an online learning mechanism based on Sparse Variational Gaussian Processes (SVGPs) with a receding-horizon control scheme. Relying solely on noisy state measurements, our approach infers a posterior distribution over the latent world policy, which is updated on streaming data via Online Variational Conditioning (OVC). The learned policy is propagated through the nonlinear world dynamics using an approximate moment propagation scheme, and fed to an uncertainty-aware Model Predictive Control (MPC), thus enabling safe maneuvering of the ego robot. The real-time feasibility and safety guarantees of SOWL-MPC are demonstrated through extensive Monte Carlo virtual experiments in ROS 2, and validated on real-world robotic hardware in an indoor arena.

</details>


### [228] [Offline Vision-Language Navigation with Geometric Goal Localization for Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22226)
*Ali Salmasi,Xianjia Yu,Tomi Westerlund*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向户外环境的完全星载视觉-语言导航(VLN)系统Edge-BehAV，通过边缘可部署小语言模型(SLM)替代云端大模型进行指令分解，并结合轻量混合语义-几何目标定位框架实现无网络依赖的自主导航。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及边缘部署、轻量化推理、目标检测等与轻量化相关的技术，但应用场景是地面机器人视觉-语言导航(VLN)，而非遥感卫星在轨目标检测。虽然"onboard inference"、"edge-deployable"、"轻量化"等概念对星上部署有间接借鉴意义，但核心任务（VLN vs. 遥感目标检测）、传感器（RGB+LiDAR vs. 遥感卫星多光谱/SAR）、目标类型（语义目标 vs. 舰船/飞机/车辆）均不匹配。

Reading priority: 2分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向弱相关，主要价值在于边缘部署和轻量化推理的benchmark方法论，但场景和任务差异过大，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视觉-语言导航(VLN)中的指令分解与目标定位。场景：户外地面机器人导航。传感器：RGB相机、LiDAR。目标类别：开放词汇语义目标（open-vocabulary objects）。

Model architecture: Edge-BehAV架构，包含：(1) 小语言模型(SLM)用于导航指令分解；(2) 轻量混合语义-几何目标定位框架（结合open-vocabulary object detection + prompted segmentation + LiDAR geometry）；(3) 视觉方位引导机制（当几何观测不可靠时）。

Lightweight method: 使用边缘可部署小语言模型(SLM)替代云端大模型（API），在3种计算平台上评估了17种SLM的精度和延迟。目标定位框架设计为轻量级（lower computational cost）。摘要未明确说明是否使用了剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等具体模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提供了部署相关信息：在3种计算平台上评估了17种SLM的延迟；最佳离线SLM比最强云端API快约9倍（approximately 9x faster）；目标定位框架将平均目标距离误差从2.05m降至0.20m且计算成本更低（lower computational cost）；完整系统在32次闭环户外试验中成功31次。但摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等具体指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：人类标注的导航指令（human-annotated instructions）。评价指标：指令分解精度（accuracy）、延迟（latency）、目标距离误差（goal-distance error）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: (1) 17种SLM vs 4种云端API的指令分解精度和延迟对比；(2) 提出的轻量混合定位框架与基线（2.05m→0.20m）的误差对比；(3) 完整Edge-BehAV系统在32次闭环户外试验中成功31次。摘要未提供消融实验细节。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向无关，场景、传感器、任务均不匹配；(2) 摘要未说明SLM的具体参数量级和是否经过量化/剪枝等压缩；(3) 未讨论在资源更受限的星上平台（如FPGA/NPU）的部署可行性；(4) 未涉及遥感图像特有的旋转框检测、小目标检测、多光谱/高光谱处理等挑战。

Ideas for my research: (1) 借鉴其"边缘可部署小模型替代云端大模型"的思路，探索在轨场景下用小模型替代大模型的可行性；(2) 其混合语义-几何定位框架中"当可靠观测不可用时保持视觉引导"的鲁棒设计思路，可迁移到遥感目标检测中处理云遮挡等退化场景；(3) 其对多种SLM在多个计算平台上的系统化benchmark方法值得借鉴，可在遥感轻量化检测中建立类似的星上部署benchmark；(4) 但需注意，本文的核心技术（VLN、open-vocabulary detection、LiDAR）与遥感目标检测差异较大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Foundation-model-based vision-language navigation (VLN) has advanced autonomous robot navigation by enabling robots to interpret natural-language instructions, identify semantic goals, and follow user-specified behavioral rules. However, existing VLN systems rely heavily on cloud-hosted foundation models for language understanding and semantic grounding, limiting their applicability where network connectivity is unavailable and reliable metric goal localization is required. Although recent small language models (SLMs) enable fully onboard inference, their suitability for navigation instruction decomposition has not been systematically evaluated. This paper makes three contributions toward fully onboard VLN for outdoor environments. First, we present the first systematic benchmark of 17 edge-deployable SLMs against 4 online APIs for robotic navigation instruction decomposition, evaluating accuracy and latency on human-annotated instructions across three computing platforms and providing practical guidance for selecting onboard language models. Second, we propose a lightweight hybrid semantic-geometric goal localization framework that combines open-vocabulary object detection, prompted segmentation, and LiDAR geometry to estimate metric goals, while maintaining visual bearing guidance when reliable geometric observations are unavailable. Third, we integrate these advances into Edge-BehAV, a fully onboard extension of the BehAV architecture that enables cloud-independent behavior-guided navigation. Experimental results show that the best offline SLM matches the instruction decomposition performance of the strongest cloud API while running approximately 9x faster and without network connectivity. The proposed goal localization framework reduces mean goal-distance error from 2.05 m to 0.20 m at lower computational cost, and the complete system succeeds in 31 of 32 closed-loop outdoor trials.

</details>


### [229] [Design and Human Evaluation of Tactile Withdrawal Reflexes for a Skin-Covered Robot Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22249)
*Laura Babayeva,Lukas Rustler,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人手臂的仿生痛觉反射机制，通过全身触觉感知实现避让运动，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是机器人触觉感知与仿生痛觉反射控制，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人仿生痛觉反射控制；应用场景：机器人手臂触觉交互；传感器类型：全身触觉皮肤（pressure-sensitive skin）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关内容。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明深度学习模型架构。系统包含：压力到疼痛增益的非线性连续映射模型、三种避让反射策略（均匀反射、生物启发式关节空间避让、笛卡尔空间沿表面法线避让）、中断-执行-返回的反射控制器。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未说明任何剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 用户研究：15名参与者；评价指标：Godspeed问卷子量表（anthropomorphism, animacy, likeability）、自定义感知自然度和安全性量表、强制选择比较、定性反馈。不涉及遥感目标检测数据集或指标。

Experiments: 用户实验比较三种反射策略。结果：均匀反射在拟人化、活力、可爱度、自然度和真实感方面评分最高，在"最安全"、"最像人类"、"最自然"方面最佳；笛卡尔反射被认为是最合适的触摸反应；生物启发式反射未在任何指标上领先。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文属于机器人触觉交互领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在技术路线、应用场景、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Nociception is a protective biological mechanism that links harmful stimulation to a reaction. This paper investigates artificial nociception for a robotic arm with whole-body tactile sensing. We present a complete pipeline that maps pressure changes from sensitive skin on a robot manipulator to bio-inspired withdrawal motions. The system first converts skin pressure into a scalar pain gain using a nonlinear continuous model. We compare three reflexes: (i) uniform reflex moves four robot joints by a fixed amount, whereby the withdrawal is approximated by a movement of the arm "toward the base", independent of where the robot was touched; (ii) biologically motivated location-dependent joint-space withdrawal derived from human withdrawal reflex characteristics; (iii) Cartesian space withdrawal along the surface normal of the contacted skin pad. All behaviors are integrated in a reflex controller that interrupts the task, executes the withdrawal, and returns to a pre-contact pose. A user study with 15 participants compared the strategies using Godspeed questionnaire subscales, custom perceived-naturalness and safety items, forced-choice comparisons, and qualitative feedback. Interestingly, participants rated more highly the uniform reflex behavior over one or both competitors on the anthropomorphism, animacy, and likeability Godspeed subscales and on the Naturalness and Realism custom scale. When asked to compare the conditions, the uniform reflex was scored best in "felt safest", "most human-like", and "most natural". This suggests that predictability of the robot behavior is key for user acceptance. The Cartesian reflex was judged the most appropriate reaction to touch. The bio-inspired reflex did not lead any evaluated measure. This may be partly attributed to the embodiment gap between the robot arm and human arm and participants having different expectations from a robot manipulator.

</details>


### [230] [A Monolithic Hand with Asymmetric Origami Bending and Dual-chamber Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22320)
*Nan Huang,Yuming Zhu,Zicong Zhang,Jianhui Liu,Xiaohuang Liu,Dihan Liu,Jiansheng Dai,Sicong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非对称折纸弯曲（AOB）和非对称双腔（ADC）设计的软体机器人手（OSOR hand），通过一体化3D打印实现弯曲运动与抓取功能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是软体机器人手的设计与制造，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文涉及软体机器人手的抓取任务，传感器类型、目标类别等均与遥感无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文涉及的是机械结构设计（AOB-S腔、AOB-D腔、手指/手掌驱动器），非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。实验为物理样机测试（弯曲角度、输出力、抓取能力），无遥感数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 实验验证了手指最大弯曲203°、输出力6.3N，手掌最大弯曲40°、输出力16N，以及抓取纸巾、水瓶等物体的能力。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文属于软体机器人领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: The passive adaptability inherent in soft robotic hands affords them advantages in applications that require safe and compliant interaction. However, existing soft robotic hands often struggle to simultaneously achieve adequate output performance and easy manufacturing due to their complicated structures. In this paper, we introduce the asymmetric origami bending (AOB) pattern for generating bending motion and the asymmetric dual-chamber (ADC) design for obtaining multifunction capability. The AOB single (AOB-S) chamber and AOB dual-chamber (AOB-D) units are designed and constitute the finger and palm actuators of the proposed Origami-inspired SOft Robotic (OSOR) hand. The OSOR hand achieves bio-inspired fingers-palm motions and adequate output performance within a monolithic structure that significantly simplifies the manufacturing process. By defining the asymmetric ratio to characterize the geometric asymmetry of the unit, the analytical models of the AOB and ADC structures are proposed. The Finite Element Analysis tool for the design of AOB actuators is obtained by geometric analysis. The asymmetric origami design grants the integrated manufacturing of the OSOR hand through a Selective Laser Sintering printing process with a single thermoplastic polyurethane material. The model and simulations are validated by experimental results. Experiments show the finger and palm maximum bending motion range of 203° and 40°, respectively, with output forces of 6.3 N and 16 N. The OSOR hand is capable of pinching a piece of tissue, stably grasping water bottles with two fingers, palm-only grasping, and completing the power grasps in the taxonomy of manufacturing grasps. The compactness, performance, and easy manufacturing of the proposed hand benefit the development of the soft robotic hand with new possibilities.

</details>


### [231] [Teachy Mini: Development and Preliminary Evaluation of a Knowledge-Based Generative Social Robot for Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22345)
*Stephan Vonschallen,Karim Kaufmann,Dominique Oberle,Friederike Eyssel,Theresa Schmiedel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大语言模型的生成式社交机器人Teachy Mini，用于高等教育个性化辅导，通过知识驱动设计实现负责任的机器人教学行为。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题，属于教育机器人/人机交互领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为高等教育个性化辅导机器人教学，场景为课堂/实验室人机交互，传感器为机器人摄像头/麦克风（非遥感传感器），目标类别为学生学习行为。

Model architecture: 基于Reachy Mini机器人平台，使用大语言模型（LLM）、检索增强生成（RAG）、状态化提示编排（stateful prompt orchestration）构建Teachy Mini系统。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验参与者N=24，进行机器人引导的学习环节。评价指标包括：负责任教学行为感知（主要指标）、系统接受度、内在动机、学习效果、客观学习收益。使用控制变量实验设计（Teachy Mini vs 非KBD对照版）。

Experiments: Teachy Mini在负责任教学行为上显著优于对照版；在个性化、幻灯片解释、苏格拉底式提问、情感支持、学习者锚定反馈方面更一致；系统接受度、内在动机、学习效果无显著组间差异；探索性分析表明KBD对客观学习收益有正向影响（考虑学习者偏好时）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法。样本量小（N=24），学习效果无显著差异，属于教育机器人领域的初步研究。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于教育机器人/人机交互领域，与遥感目标检测、模型轻量化、星上部署等研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Generative social robots (GSRs) powered by large language models offer new possibilities for personalized tutoring in higher education, but also introduce risks related to misinformation, missing transparency, or reinforcing incorrect student responses. Prior work identified knowledge-based design (KBD) requirements that define the informational prerequisites for GSRs to manifest responsible and effective tutoring behavior in higher education. In this paper, we operationalized selected KBD requirements in the Reachy Mini robot platform through system prompting, retrieval-augmented generation, and stateful prompt orchestration. As a result, we present Teachy Mini, a GSR tutoring system that was developed using KBD. To test the system, we conducted a preliminary evaluation study. Participants (N = 24) completed a robot-guided learning session about research methodologies. They learned either with Teachy Mini or with a control version that did not follow KBD principles. Teachy Mini was perceived as significantly more aligned with responsible tutoring behavior than the control robot. Moreover, a manipulation check illustrated that Teachy Mini used personalization, slide-grounded explanations, Socratic questioning, affective support, and learner-anchored feedback more consistently than the control robot. No significant between-condition differences were found in system acceptance, intrinsic motivation, or learning effectiveness, although exploratory analyses suggested a positive effect of KBD on objective learning gains when accounting for learner preferences. Overall, the study offered an initial implementation and preliminary evaluation of KBD for GSR tutoring, indicating that KBD can shape responsible robot behavior and potentially increase learning effectiveness in robot-supported learning.

</details>


### [232] [Conformal Constraint Tightening for Chance-Constrained Motion Planning with Unknown Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22409)
*Shubham Natraj,Bruno Sinopoli,Yiannis Kantaros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于保形预测(conformal prediction)的约束收紧方法，使现有运动规划器在未知真实动力学系统上也能获得概率性的任务完成保证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人运动规划(motion planning)中的动力学不确定性问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。方法为planner-agnostic（与规划器无关）的约束收紧流程，可适配采样规划或深度强化学习等现有规划器。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：任务完成率(task completion)、概率性任务完成保证(probabilistic task-completion guarantee)。

Experiments: 在真实系统上验证了理论保证，相比仅使用名义模型(nominal model)的规划方法，任务完成率有显著提升。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制/运动规划领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Motion planning algorithms compute control sequences that drive autonomous robots to goal regions while avoiding unsafe states. Existing methods, from sampling-based planning to deep reinforcement learning, typically provide task-completion guarantees only with respect to a nominal model or simulator, which may be invalidated when the true dynamics are unknown or difficult to model accurately. This letter addresses this limitation for systems with unknown dynamics and an available approximate nominal model, contributing a planner-agnostic constraint-tightening procedure that equips existing planners with a probabilistic task-completion guarantee on the true system. We leverage conformal prediction to provide a probabilistic bound on the nominal-to-true trajectory deviation over a distribution of planning problems. We tighten the planning constraints using that bound, and show that solving the tightened problem under the nominal model is a sufficient condition for solving the original problem on the true system with a prescribed probability. We validate the theoretical guarantees empirically and demonstrate substantially improved task completion relative to nominal-model planning.

</details>


### [233] [Robot Learning to Communicate through Projected Visual Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22434)
*Danyang Yan,Boyuan Wang,Jiaxun Liu,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用21自由度灵巧手和可微分阴影自模型实现机器人动态阴影表达的系统，通过梯度优化生成手影手势、手语和动物模仿，用于机器人视觉通信。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、星上部署等模型压缩或边缘部署技术。论文属于机器人视觉表达与可微分渲染领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人阴影表达（shadow expression）、手影生成、手势通信。应用场景：机器人视觉通信、手影表演、手语表达。传感器类型：未明确说明，涉及投影和视觉感知。目标类别：手影形状、手势、动物轮廓。摘要未说明遥感相关任务。

Model architecture: 21自由度灵巧手（dexterous hand）配合柔性软皮肤（compliant soft skin）；可微分阴影自模型（differentiable shadow self-model），学习手部构型到投影阴影外观的映射。摘要未说明网络具体结构（如CNN/Transformer等）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理能力，也未提及星上/边缘部署相关硬件（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明标准数据集和定量评价指标。实验包括仿真和物理实验，展示手语手势、手影木偶戏、动物运动模仿等定性结果。

Experiments: 仿真和物理实验验证了阴影表达系统在手语手势、手影木偶戏、动物运动模仿上的效果。摘要未提供定量对比实验或消融实验的数值结果。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感目标检测或轻量化方面的局限性分析。论文本身局限：仅针对阴影表达这一特定任务，未讨论通用性、实时性、鲁棒性等。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Humans routinely communicate through abstractions of their bodies, including shadows, silhouettes, and reflections. Yet robots remain largely confined to expressing themselves through their physical morphology. Enabling robots to communicate through such projected visual abstractions requires reasoning not only about bodily motion but also about how that motion is transformed into an external representation perceived by an observer. Among these abstractions, shadows provide a particularly compelling example because they emerge directly from the robot's embodiment while remaining visually distinct from the body itself. Here, we present a robotic system capable of dynamic shadow expression using a 21-degree-of-freedom dexterous hand with compliant soft skin and a learned shadow self-model. The soft-skinned embodiment reduces light leakage to produce visually continuous silhouettes, while the differentiable self-model learns the mapping between hand configurations and projected shadow appearance through task-agnostic self-exploration. Given a target shadow image or video, the robot optimizes its hand configurations through gradient-based search over 1 the learned self-model and refines the solution through collision-aware simulation to obtain physically feasible motions. For dynamic shadow performance, we further introduce expressive-region objectives, temporal smoothness regularization, and keyframe-based optimization to preserve visually important motion cues while reducing optimization complexity. We demonstrate robotic shadow expression across sign-language gestures, hand-shadow puppetry, and animal motion imitation in both simulation and physical experiments. These results establish a framework for enabling robots to manipulate projected visual abstractions of themselves for communication and visual storytelling.

</details>


### [234] [Plug, Play, and Comply: A Modular Framework for Online Variable Impedance with Arbitrarily Oriented Compliance Axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22483)
*Mihael Simonič,Xiaocong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个与机器人无关的柔顺控制框架，扩展ROS控制生态，支持标准化关节和笛卡尔命令接口，实现跨机械臂平台的可移植柔顺控制。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是机器人柔顺控制框架（compliant-control framework），属于机器人控制软件工程领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人柔顺控制（compliant control）；应用场景：接触丰富的操作任务（contact-rich manipulation）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 不涉及深度学习模型。提出的是基于插件的控制软件架构（plugin-based architecture），包含通用包装器（generic wrappers）和运行时加载的插件，使用Pinocchio库从URDF模型计算机器人运动学和动力学。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 不涉及遥感或目标检测数据集。实验方式：真实机器人实验（real-robot experiments）验证任务依赖的柔顺性；仿真实验（simulations）验证跨机械臂可移植性。

Experiments: 真实机器人实验展示了接触丰富操作中的任务依赖柔顺控制；仿真实验展示了在不同运动学和动力学特性的机械臂上的可移植性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何相关局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于机器人控制软件工程方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: The paper proposes a robot-agnostic compliant-control framework that extends the ROS control ecosystem with standardized joint and Cartesian command interfaces. It addresses a key limitation of existing control software: no reusable infrastructure for implementing compliant-control algorithms across different manipulators while preserving a common interface to higher-level applications. A plugin-based architecture separates controller infrastructure from control-law implementation. Generic wrappers use existing hardware abstractions to interface with different manipulators, while runtime-loaded plugins implement only the control law. Command interfaces support joint- and Cartesian-space references, stiffness and damping gains, nullspace targets, and feedforward terms, enabling variable impedance and diverse compliant-control formulations. Robot kinematics and dynamics are computed from URDF models using Pinocchio. The architecture facilitates the development of compliant-control strategies and enables the same implementation to be deployed across platforms unchanged. The complete framework, including reference controllers, high-level task interfaces, and example configurations for various manipulators, is open-sourced. The reference Cartesian impedance controller supports task-dependent compliance by rotating translational and rotational stiffness and damping, allowing the principal compliance directions to be updated online according to local task geometry rather than remaining fixed in the robot base or TCP frame. This is particularly important in contact-rich manipulation, where the desired directions of motion, constraints, and compliance directions may vary throughout task execution. Real-robot experiments demonstrate task-dependent compliance in contact-rich manipulation, while simulations show portability across manipulators with distinct kinematic and dynamic characteristics.

</details>


### [235] [ViTacWorld: Scaling Visuo-Tactile World Models for Contact-Rich Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22530)
*Yunao Huang,Shiyu Sang,Haotao Lu,Suting Ni,Shijie Wu,Ziyang Guo,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ViTacWorld，一个面向接触式机器人操作的视觉-触觉世界模型，用于生成视觉-触觉-动作轨迹并评估策略，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。论文聚焦于机器人操作中的视觉-触觉世界模型，属于机器人学习领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：接触式机器人操作（contact-rich robot manipulation）；场景：机器人抓取与操作；传感器：视觉相机+触觉传感器；目标类别：无目标检测任务。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: ViTacWorld是一个动作条件化的视觉-触觉世界模型（action-conditioned visuo-tactile world model），利用真实触觉数据集和仿真环境进行大规模预训练，再通过真实策略rollout微调。模型预测与动作对齐的视觉观测和触觉反馈。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用公开真实触觉数据集和自建仿真环境。评价指标：物理合理性（physically meaningful rollouts）、策略性能提升（policy performance improvement）、动作条件化策略评估能力。具体数据集名称和指标数值摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在接触式操作任务上验证了ViTacWorld能生成物理有意义的rollout，通过可扩展数据增强提升策略性能，并支持动作条件化策略评估。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或遥感检测方法。触觉传感器在遥感卫星场景中不适用。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人操作领域的世界模型方法，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题方向不匹配。

Abstract: Contact-rich robot manipulation requires physical interaction cues that are often invisible to cameras, making tactile sensing essential for robust control. However, scaling visuo-tactile robot learning remains difficult because real tactile interaction data are expensive to collect, hardware-dependent, and limited in task and scene diversity. We present ViTacWorld, an action-conditioned visuo-tactile world model for scalable contact-rich robot manipulation. ViTacWorld leverages public real tactile datasets and a constructed simulation environment to scale visuo-tactile-action data, exploiting the fact that tactile signals are directly grounded in physical contact and can exhibit a smaller simulation-to-real gap than purely visual observations. The model is first pretrained with large-scale real and simulated visuo-tactile trajectories, and then finetuned with real-world policy rollouts to better match downstream manipulation behaviors. Given robot actions, ViTacWorld predicts temporally aligned visual observations and tactile feedback, enabling visuo-tactile-action rollout generation. To the best of our knowledge, ViTacWorld is the first framework that uses a world model for robot visuo-tactile-action trajectory generation and policy evaluation. It serves two roles: synthesizing rollouts to improve downstream tactile policies, and evaluating policies by predicting action-conditioned visuo-tactile outcomes under controlled action sequences. Experiments on contact-rich manipulation tasks show that ViTacWorld generates physically meaningful rollouts, improves policy performance through scalable data augmentation, and enables action-conditioned policy evaluation. Project page: https://vitacworld.github.io/

</details>


### [236] [Robot-Factored World Models via Robot Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22535)
*Byungjun Kim,Taeksoo Kim,Hyunsoo Cha,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"机器人分解世界模型"(robot-factored world models)，通过将机器人动作实现和渲染过程从世界模型中剥离，利用标称轨迹和URDF渲染的机器人几何信息作为视觉接口，提升视频预测和跨机器人泛化能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人操作场景下的视频世界模型，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频世界模型（video world model）用于未来观测预测；应用场景：机器人操作（robotics manipulation）；传感器类型：RGB/depth相机；目标类别：机器人本体、操作物体。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 提出robot-factored world model，核心设计包括：(1) 动作实现分解——通过机器人控制器和运动学将动作命令滚动为标称轨迹(nominal trajectory)；(2) 机器人渲染——通过URDF渲染机器人几何信息，与静态RGB/depth上下文构成共享视觉接口。模型输入为渲染的机器人几何+场景RGB/depth，输出为未来视频帧预测。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向机器人操作场景，未考虑星上/边缘部署需求。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。实验部分提到与vector-conditioned baselines对比，并测试了跨机器人泛化能力。

Experiments: 实验表明：(1) 渲染接口方法优于vector-conditioned基线；(2) 能泛化到未见过的机器人本体；(3) 能从人类演示中通过重定向和渲染生成机器人操作视频。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 未涉及任何目标检测任务；(3) 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；(4) 方法依赖URDF和机器人运动学模型，无法直接迁移到遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：(1) 将领域先验知识（如卫星轨道动力学、传感器模型）显式建模并"分解"出网络，减少网络学习负担的思路，与轻量化中"用先验替代网络容量"的思想有相通之处；(2) 渲染几何作为中间表示的思路，在遥感中可类比为将地理坐标/投影变换等几何信息显式编码，辅助检测模型理解空间关系。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models predict future observations from an initial observation and an action signal. In robotics, actions influence future observations through two distinct processes: they are first realized into robot motion by the robot body and controller, and the scene then responds through contact and object motion. Conditioning directly on action commands asks the world model to learn the realization process itself, while conditioning on logged future states leaks the interaction outcomes it is meant to predict. We propose robot-factored world models, which move two robot-specific factors outside the world model. First, action realization: each command is rolled through the robot's own controller and kinematics into a deployment-available nominal trajectory, a middle signal that avoids both action-realization learning and future-state leakage. Second, robot rendering: this nominal trajectory is rendered through the robot URDF, factoring the robot's geometry, kinematics, and appearance out of the model and into explicit rendered robot geometry. To resolve depth ambiguity, we pair end-effector depth with scene depth, giving geometric cues for contact and occlusion beyond image-plane overlap. Together, camera-aware static RGB/depth context and rendered robot geometry form a shared visual world-model interface that stays consistent across viewpoints and robot embodiments, so the model sees the action only as visible robot geometry and learns how objects respond to it. Our experiments show that the rendered interface outperforms vector-conditioned baselines and generalizes to unseen robot embodiments at inference. We further demonstrate that our model generates robot manipulation videos from human demonstrations by retargeting and rendering the hand motion as robot geometry.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [The Lift Spectrum: How Measurement-to-Space Adaptivity Shapes Robustness in Image-Free Single-Pixel Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22077)
*Yuyuan Han,Jingwei Li,Long Qiu,Chong Wang,Wenxuan Hao,Jiangyu Han,Xinyu Yao,Yuchen He,Hui Chen,Jianbin Liu,Huaibin Zheng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单像素感知的图像免重建方法STSF+TPLS，将1D测量序列到2D表示的"提升"（lift）作为核心设计轴，通过探针选择的循环编码器、交叉注意力提升模块和任务优先损失调度，在极低采样率下实现分割任务，并迁移到真实单像素硬件平台。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及单像素成像（single-pixel sensing）和图像免重建推理（image-free inference），属于计算成像与压缩感知领域，并非遥感/卫星场景。虽然涉及目标分割（非检测）、轻量推理（14ms/mask）和硬件部署，但场景是单像素相机而非遥感卫星在轨平台，传感器类型（单像素而非光学/SAR/高光谱遥感器）和应用目标（通用场景分割而非遥感目标检测）均不匹配。

Reading priority: 1分——与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。虽然涉及轻量推理和硬件部署，但场景（单像素成像）、任务（分割而非检测）、传感器（单像素而非遥感器）均不匹配，且未涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏等核心轻量化方法。除非对免重建推理或极低采样率下的鲁棒性有特殊兴趣，否则阅读优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语义分割（semantic segmentation），非目标检测（object detection）。应用场景：单像素成像（single-pixel imaging）下的图像免重建推理。传感器类型：单像素探测器（single-pixel detector）。目标类别：摘要未说明具体类别，使用三个数据集（未具名）的foreground mIoU评估。

Model architecture: STSF (Spatiotemporal Soft-Fusion) 网络：探针选择的循环编码器（probe-selected recurrent encoder）+ 交叉注意力提升模块（cross-attention lift）+ U-Net++ 解码器。TPLS (Task-Prioritized Loss Scheduling)：任务优先损失调度策略，引入调度化的重建先验（scheduled reconstruction prior）。

Lightweight method: 轻量化主要体现在极低采样率（3.13%甚至0.39%）下的高效推理，而非传统意义上的模型压缩（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）。模型本身设计为免重建（image-free），跳过了图像重建步骤，减少了计算开销。在真实单像素平台上达到约14ms/mask的推理速度。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存等具体轻量化指标。

Onboard deployability: 在真实单像素实验平台上进行了概念验证（proof of concept），无需微调即可迁移，推理速度约14ms/mask。但这是单像素硬件平台，非星上/边缘部署场景。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等星上部署关键指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用三个数据集（摘要未具名），评价指标为foreground mIoU（前景平均交并比）。采样率设置：3.13%和0.39%。在噪声条件下进行鲁棒性评估。摘要未说明数据集规模、类别数等细节。

Experiments: 在3.13%采样率下，STSF+TPLS超越此前image-free基线方法，foreground mIoU提升+3.2到+9.9个百分点。在0.39%极低采样率下性能趋于稳定。无噪声条件下，reconstruct-then-segment基线最优；但在校准测量噪声下，image-free推理反超，原因是重建管线会放大测量噪声。分析了三种失败模式：collapse（坍塌）、imprinting（印记）、coarsening（粗化）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向基本无关，场景、传感器、任务均不匹配。2. 仅针对分割任务，未涉及目标检测（尤其是旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感核心问题）。3. 仅在单像素硬件上验证，未在遥感卫星平台或嵌入式设备（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）上部署。4. 摘要未说明模型参数量、FLOPs等计算资源需求。5. 数据集未具名，难以评估其在遥感场景下的泛化能力。

Ideas for my research: 1. **免重建推理思路**：在遥感在轨场景中，如果传感器资源受限（如高光谱/多光谱波段数有限），可借鉴"从原始测量直接推理"的思想，跳过图像重建步骤，减少星上计算和存储开销。2. **极低采样率下的鲁棒性**：论文展示了在3.13%甚至0.39%采样率下仍能保持性能，这对星上带宽受限场景有启发意义。3. **噪声鲁棒性分析**：论文揭示了重建管线会放大测量噪声，这一发现对遥感在轨场景中传感器噪声处理有参考价值。4. **交叉注意力提升模块**：将1D测量映射到2D表示的cross-attention lift设计，可借鉴用于遥感多模态特征融合。5. **TPLS损失调度**：任务优先的损失调度策略可借鉴用于遥感检测任务中的多任务学习。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为场景差异大。

Abstract: Single-pixel sensing encodes a scene as a short sequence of coded measurements, and image-free methods infer the task directly from that sequence. Removing reconstruction does not remove the difficulty: it relocates it to the lift, the map from 1D measurements to a 2D representation, which prior work treats as a trivial reshape. We recast the lift as the central design axis of image-free sensing and order methods by how strongly it adapts to its input: a fixed-physics inverse (reconstruct-then-segment), a learned static projection, or a content-adaptive retrieval; position on this lift spectrum predicts behavior as acquisition degrades. The spatiotemporal soft-fusion (STSF) network pairs a probe-selected recurrent encoder with a cross-attention lift chosen by a parameter-matched ablation, ahead of its U-Net++ decoder, and trains under task-prioritized loss scheduling (TPLS), a scheduled reconstruction prior. In simulation, STSF+TPLS surpasses the prior image-free baseline on three datasets at 3.13% sampling (+3.2 to +9.9 pp foreground mIoU) and plateaus down to 0.39%. The strongest clean-trained reconstruct-then-segment baseline wins the noiseless limit, but under calibrated measurement noise image-free inference overtakes it, for a measured reason: the reconstruction pipeline amplifies the identical measurement noise before its segmenter reads it. Each region fails in its own signature: collapse, imprinting, or coarsening. STSF+TPLS transfers without fine-tuning to a real single-pixel bench as a proof of concept, at about 14 ms per mask. Charting the lift turns a scattered design space into a map of which lift to deploy at each operating point. Code and pretrained weights: https://github.com/Hanyuyuan6/STSF-TPLS

</details>
